------------------------------------------------------------------------

ilakkaṇac curukkam
mutalāvatu: eḻuttatikāram

1.1. eḻuttiyal

ilakkaṇa nūlāvatu, uyarntōr vaḻakkattiyuñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkattiyum aṟinnu vitippaṭi
eḻutuvataṟkum pēcutaṟkuṅ karuviyākiya nūlām.
-----

2. annūl eḻuttatikāram, collatikāram, toṭarmoḻiyatikāram eṉa, mūṉṟatitikāraṅkaḷāka
vakukkappaṭum.
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar
3. eḻuttāvatu collukku mutaṟkāraṇamākiya oliyām
-----

4. avveḻuttu, uyireḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu, uyir meyyeḻuttu, āytaveḻuttu eṉa
nāṉku vakippaṭum.
-----

5. uyireḻuttukkaḷ, a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, e, ē, ai, o, ō, oḷa eṉṉum
paṉṉiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷumām. ivi āvi eṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

6. uyireḻuttukkaḷ, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, eṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.
-----

7. kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ, a, i, u, e, o eṉṉum aintumām. ivi kuṟil eṉavum peyar
peṟum.
-----

8. neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, oḷa eṉṉum ēḻamām. ivi neṭil eṉavum peyar
peṟum
-----

9. meyyeḻuttukkaḷ, k, ṅ, c, ñ, ṭ, ṇ, t, n, p, m, y, r, l, v, ḻ, ḷ, ṟ, ṉ
eṉṉum patiṉeṭṭeḻuttukkaḷumākum.
ivi uṭal, uṭampu, uṟuppu, oṟṟu, puḷḷi eṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

10. meyyeḻuttukkaḷ, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.
-----

11. valleḻuttukkaḷ, k, c, ṭ, t, p, ṟ, eṉṉum āṟumām.
ivi valliṉam, vaṉkaṇam, vali eṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

12. melleḻuttukkaḷ, ṅ, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ eṉṉum āṟumām.
ivi melliṉam, meṉkaṇam, meli eṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

13. iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ, y, r, l, v, ḻ, ḷ eṉṉum āṟumām.
ivi iṭiyiṉam, iṭikkaṇam, iṭi eṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

14. a, i, u eṉṉum mūṉṟum, moḻikku mutalilē cuṭṭup poruḷil varumpōtu,
cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷām. utāraṇam.
avaṉ, ivaṉ, uvaṉ,
akkoṟṟaṉ, ikkoṟṟaṉ, ukkoṟṟaṉ.
-----

15.ekaram moḻikku mutalilum, akaramum okāramum moḻikku kaṭiyilum, viṉāpporuḷil
varum pōtu, viṉāveḻuttukkaḷām.utāraṇam.
evaṉ, ekkoṟṟaṉ
koṟṟāṉa, koṟṟaṉō
ēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉē
yā eṉṉum uyir meyyum, moḻikku mutalilē viṉāp poruḷil varum pōtu
viṉāveḻuttām
-----

16. akarattukku ākāramum, ikarattukku īkāramum, okarattukku ōkāramum, ukarattukku
ūkāramum, ekarattukku ēkāramum, aikārattukku ikaramum, okarattukku ōkāramum,
oḷakārattukku ukaramum, kakarattukku ṅakaramum, cakarattukku ñakaramum, ṭakarattukku
ṇakaramum, takarattukku nakaramum, pakarattukku makaramum, ṟakarattukku ṉakaramum, iṉa
veḻuttukkaḷām. iṭiyeḻuttāṟum. ōriṉamākum; avi ivviraṇṭōriṉamākāvām.
-----

17. uyir meyyeḻuttukkaḷāvaṉa. puṉṉiraṇṭuyirum patiṉeṭṭu meymēlun taṉittaṉi
ēṟivarutalākiya irunāṟṟuppatiṉāṟumām.
avi, ka, kā, ki, kī mutaliyavikaḷām.
uyir meykkuṟṟeḻuttut toṇṇūṟu; uyirmey neṭṭeḻuttu nūṟṟirupattāṟu; āka
uyirmey irunāṟṟup patiṉāṟu.
ūyirmey valleḻuttu eḻupattiraṇṭu, uyirmey melleḻuttu eḻupattiraṇṭu, uyirmey
yiṭiyeḻuttu eḻupattiraṇṭu, āka uyirmey irunāṟṟup patiṉāṟu.
-----

18. āytaveḻuttāvatu, kuṟṟeḻuttukkum uyirmey valleḻuttukkum naṭuvē mūṉṟu
puḷḷi vaṭivuṭiyatāy varum ōreḻuttākum.
utāraṇam. eḵku, kaḵcu, aḵtu, paḵṟi
-----

19. mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, meypatiṉeṭṭum, uyirmey irunāṟṟup
patiṉāṟum, āytam oṉṟum ākiya irunāṟṟu nāṟpattēḻeḻuttukkaḷun tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil
vaḻaṅki varutal kaṇṭu koḷka.
------

-----

eḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttiri
20. kuṟṟeḻuttukku māttiri oṉṟu, neṭṭeḻuttukku māttiri iraṇṭu.
meyyeḻuttukkum āytaveḻuttukkun taṉittaṉi māttiri ari.
ūyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttukku ēṟiya uyiriṉa ḷavākiya māttiri oṉṟu; uyirmey
neṭṭeḻuttukku ēṟiya uyiriṉaḷavākiya māttiri iraṇṭu.
māttiriyāvatu kaṇṇimippoḻutu, allatu kinnoṭippoḻutu.
-----

21. uyireḻuttukkuḷḷē, ukaramum ikaramum, cilaviṭaṅkaḷilē tam māttiriyiṟ kuṟivāka
olittu niṟkum. āvvukarattiṟku kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟum peyarām.
-----

22. kuṟṟiyalukaramāvatu, taṉik kuṟṟeḻuttallāta maṟṟiyeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṉṉē
moḻikaḷiḷiṟutiyil valliṉa meykaḷil ēṟi niṟkum ukaramākum.
ākkuṟṟiyalukaram, īṟṟeḻuttākiya taṉṉittoṭarkiṉṟa ayaleḻuttiṉ vakiyiṉālē, neṭiṟ
ṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram, āytattoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram, uyirttoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram,
vaṉṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram, meṉṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram, iṭittoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram, eṉa
āṟuvakippaṭum. avikaḷuḷ, neṭiṟṟoṭar māttiram iraṇṭeḻuttu moḻiyākiyum,
maṟṟiyintu toṭarum mūṉṟeḻuttu mutaliya pala veḻuttu moḻiyākiyum varum.
utāraṇam.

nāku, āṭu

neṭiṟṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram

eḵku, kaḵcu

āytattoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram

varaku, palācu

uyirttoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram

kokku, kaccu

vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram

caṅku, vaṇṭu

meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram

alku, eytu

iṭittoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram

-----

23. taṉikkuṟṟaṟeḻuttukkuppiṉ valliṉa meykaḷil ēṟi niṟkum ukaramum, melliṉa
meykaḷil ēṟi niṟkum ukaramum muṟṟiyalukaramām.
utāraṇam. naku, kocu, kaṭu, atu, kaṇu, tiru, vaḻu: pūṇu, vāru, urumu, katavu,
nellu, koḷḷu.
-----

24. kuṟṟiyalikaramāvatu, yakaram vantu puṇarumiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaran tirinta ikaramām.
utāraṇam.

nāku + yātu =

nākiyatu

eḵku + yātu =

e.kiyātu

varaku + yātu =

varakiyātu

kokku + yātu =

kokkiyātu

caṅku + yātu =

caṅkiyātu

alku + yātu =

alkiyātu

aṉṟiyum, miyāveṉṉum aciccollilē makarattiṉ mēl ēṟi niṟkum ikaramuṅ
kuṟṟiyalukaran tirinta ikaramām.
-----

25. pāṭṭil ōci kuṟintaviṭattu, uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷḷum, oṟṟiyeḻuttukkuḷḷum,
cilacila, tam māttirikaḷiṉ atikamāka olikkum, avivuyireḻuttukku uyiraḷapeṭi
eṉṟum peyarām.
-----

26. uyiraḷapeṭiyāvaṉa, moḻikku mutalilāyiṉum iṭiyilāyiṉuṅ kaṭiyilāyiṉun tam
māttiriyiṉ atikamāka olittu varukiṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷēḻumām. āḷapeṭukiṉṟa
neṭṭeḻuttukkup piṉ ataṟkiṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttu aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭum.
utāraṇam.

āaṭi,

īiṭu,

ūumi,

ēeṭu,

aiiyam,

ōoṭu,

oḷuvi,

palāa.

cila viṭayaṅkaḷilē kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttākip piṉṉaḷapeṭukkum.utāraṇam.
eḻutal - eḻūutal,
varum - varūum,
kuri - kuriui
-----

27. oṟṟaḷapeṭiyāvaṉa, moḻikku iṭiyilāyiṉuṅ kaṭiyilāyiṉun tam māttiriyiṉ
atikamāka olittu varukiṉṟa ṅ, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ, v, y, l, ḷ eṉṉum pattu meykaḷum
āytamumām. āḷapeṭukiṉṟa oṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉ av voṟṟoḻuttē aṟikuṟiyāka
eḻutappaṭum. ivvoṟṟaḷapeṭi, kuṟiṟkīḻuṅ kuṟaliṇikkīḻum varum.
utāraṇam.
caṅṅku, piññcu, kaṇṇṭam, panntu, ammpu, aṉṉpu, tevvvar, meyyyar, cellka, koḷḷka,
eḵḵku, araṅṅku, aṅṅkaṉinta, maṭaṅkalanta.
-----

28. kuṟṟiyalukarattukkuṅ, kuṟṟiyalikarattukkun taṉittaṉi māttiri ari,
uyirupeṭikku māttiri mūṉṟu, oṟṟaḷapeṭikku māttiri oṉṟu.
-----

29. paṇṭamāṟṟalilum, aḻittalilum, pulampalilum, irākattilum, uyireḻuttum,
meyyeḻuttum, tamakkuc colliya aḷavi kaṭantu nīṇṭolikkum.
-----

------

mutaṉili
30. paṉṉiraṇṭuyireḻuttukkaḷum, uyirēṟiya ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma, va, ya, ña eṉṉum
oṉpatu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum, moḻikku mutalil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
aṇi, āṭi, ili, īral, ural, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oḷi, ōṭu, oḷavi.
kari, cari, naṉmi, pantu, maṇi, vayal, yamaṉ, ñamali.
------

31. ivikaḷuḷḷē, ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma, eṉṉum āṟu meykaḷum, paṉṉiraṇṭuyirōṭum
moḻikku mutalāki varum.
utāraṇam.

----

32. vakaramey, a, ā, i, ī, e, ē, ai, oḷa eṉṉum eṭṭuyirōṭu, moḻikku mutalāki
varum.
utāraṇam.
vaḷi, vāḷi, viḷi, vīṭu, veṇmi, vēli, viyam, veḷavāl.
----

33. yakaramey, a, ā, u, ū, o, oḷa ṉe;ṉum āṟayirōṭu, moḻikku mutalākivarum.
utāraṇam.
yavaṉar, yāṉi, yukam, yūkam, yōkam, yuvaṉam.
----

34. ñakaramey, a, ā, e, o, eṉṉum nāṉkuyirōṭu moḻikku mutalāki varum.
utāraṇam.
ñamali, ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkal.

----

iṟuti nili
35. ekaram oḻinta patiṉōruyirkaḷum, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ, y, r, l, v, ḻ, ḷ, eṉṉum
patiṉoru meykaḷumākiya irupattiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷum, moḻikkiṟutiyil niṟkum
eḻuttukkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
viḷa, palā, kiḷi, tī, kaṭu, pū, cē, ki, no, pō, veḷa, uriñ, maṇ, verin, maram,
poṉ, kāy, vēr, vēl, tev, yāḻ, vāḷ.

----

eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cāriyi
36. uyirneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ kārāccāriyi peṟṟum, avikaḷuḷ, ai oḷa, iraṇṭuṅ
kārāccāriyi yēyaṉṟik kāṉcāriyiyum peṟum.
utāraṇam.
ākāram, īkāram, ūkāram, ēkāram, aikāram, okāram, oḷakāram, aikāṉ, oḷakāṉ.
ūyirkkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum, uyirmeykkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum, karam, kāram, kāṉ, eṉṉum
mūṉṟu cāriyi peṟum.
utāraṇam.
akaram, akāram, aḵkāṉ, kakaram, kakāram, kaḵkāṉ.
mēyyeḻuttukkaḷ, a eṉṉuñ cāriyi ataṉōṭu karam, kāram, kāṉ eṉṉum cāriyi peṟum.
utāraṇam.
ka, ṅa, kakaram, kakāram, kaḵkāṉ, ṅakaram, ṅakāram. ṅaḵkāṉ.
uyirmey neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, cāriyi peṟum meykaḷ cāriyi peṟātum iyaṅkāvām.

---

pōliyeḻuttukkaḷ
37. akarattōṭu yakaramey cērntu aikāram pōṉṟum. akarattōṭu vakaramey cērntu oḷakāram,
pōṉṟum olikkum.
utāraṇam. aiyaṉ - ayyaṉ; oḷavi - avvi.

ēḻuttiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. 2. pataviyal

38. patamāvatu, ta oreḻuttālāyiṉum iraṇṭu mutaliya palaveḻuttukkaḷāyiṉum ākkappaṭṭup
poruḷi aṟippatām. ātu, pakāppatamum, pakupatamum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
---

39. pakāppatamāvatu, pakukkapaṭāta iyalpuṭiya patamām. ātu, peyarppakāppatam,
viṉippakāppatam, iṭip pakāppatam, urip pakāppatam, eṉa nāṉku vakippaṭum.
utāraṇam.
nilam, nīr, maram peyarp pakāppatam
naṭa, vā, uṇ viṉip pakāppatam
maṟṟu, ē, ō iṭip pakāppatam
uṟu, tava, naṉi urip pakāppatam
----

40. paṉupatamāvatu, pakukkappaṭum iyalpiyuṭiya patamām. atu, peyarppakupatam,
viṉippakupatam, eṉa iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟul, viṉip pakupatam, terinili viṉip
pakupatam, kuṟippu viṉip pakupatamum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
utāraṇam.
poṉṉaṉ .. peyarp pakupatam
naṭantāṉ .. terinili viṉip pakupatam
periyaṉ .. urippakāppatam

----

teriniliviṉiyuṅ kuṟippu viṉiyum pakupatamākum eṉavē, avviruvaki viṉiyālaṇiyum
peyarkaḷum pakupatamākum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
pakutavuṟuppu
41. pakupatattukku uṟuppukkaḷ, pakuti, vikuti, iṭinili cāriyi, canti, vikāram,
eṉa āṟām. pukupatam, i;vvāṟuṟuppukkaḷuḷḷum pakuti vikuti eṉṉum iraṇṭu
mutaliyavavikaḷiṉāl muṭivu peṟum.
utāraṇam.

----

pakuti
42. pakutikaḷāvaṉa, pakupataṅkaḷiṉ mutalilē niṟkum pakāppataṅkaḷām.
---

43. peyarppakupataṅkaḷukkup perumpālum poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil,
eṉṉum āṟavakip peyarccoṟkaḷum, ciṟupāṉmi cuṭṭiṭic coṟkaḷ, viṉāviṭic coṟkaḷ,
piṟa maṟṟu eṉṉum iṭiccoṟkaḷum, pakutikaḷāy varum.
utāraṇam.
(1) poṉṉaṉ, nilattaṉ, tiyāṉ, pallaṉ, kariyaṉ, naṭiyaṉ.
(2) avaṉ, ivaṉ, uvaṉ, evaṉ, ēvaṉ, yāvaṉ, piṟaṉ, maṟṟiyaṉ.
---

44. viṉikkuṟippu pakupataṅkaḷukku, mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉavākiya aṟuvakip peyarccoṟkaḷum,
iṭiccoṟkaḷum, pakutikaḷāy varum.
utāraṇam.
(1) poṉṉaṉ, nilattaṉ, tiyāṉ, pallaṉ, kariyaṉ, naṭiyaṉ.
(2) āṟṟu, iṟṟu, eṟṟu
---

45. mi vikuti puṇarntu niṉṟa cemmi karumi mutaliya paṇpup peyarkaḷ, vikuti
puṇarumpoḻutu, perumpālum vikārappaṭṭu varum. ivi vikārappaṭutal patappuṇarccikkuṅ
koḷka.
utāraṇam.
aṇiyaṉ: iṅkē aṇimiyiṉ mi vikuti keṭṭatu.
kariyaṉ: iṅkē karumiyiṉ mivikuti keṭṭu, naṭu ukaram ikaramāyt tirintatu.
pāci: iṅmē pacumiyiṉ mivikuti keṭṭu, mutal nīṇṭatu.
pēraṟivu: iṅmē perumiyiṉ mivikutiyōṭu naṭu niṉṟa ukaravuyir keṭṭu mutal nīṇṭatu.
kuruṅkutiri: iṅkē karumiyiṉ mivikuti keṭṭu, varum valleḻuttiṟku
iṉamelleḻuttu mikuntatu.
pintar: iṅkē pakamiyiṉ mi vikutiyōṭu naṭu niṉṟa kakaravuyir mey keṭṭu, mutalakaram
aikāramāy tirintu, varum valleḻuttukku iṉa melleḻuttu mikunnatu.
veṟṟili: iṅkē veṟumiyiṉ mivikuti keṭṭu, naṭu niṉṟa mey iraṭṭittatu.
cētāmpal: iṅkē cemmiyiṉ mivikuti keṭṭu, mutal nīṇṭu, naṭu niṉṟa, makaramey
takarameyyāyt tirintatu.
---

46. teriniliviṉip pakupataṅkaḷukkup perumpālum naṭa vā mutaliya viṉiccoṟkaḷum,
ciṟupāṉmi peyarccol iṭiccol uriccoṟkaḷum, pakutikaḷāy varum.
utāraṇam.
naṭa, naṭantāṉ viṉiyaṭi
vā, vantāṉ
nil, niṉṟāṉ
kāṇ, kaṇṭāṉ
cittiram, cittirittāṉ peyaraṭi
kaṭikkaṇ, kaṭikkaṇittāṉ
pōl, poṉpōṉṟāṉ iṭiyaṭi
nikar, pulinikarttāṉ
cāl, cāṉṟāṉ uriyaṭi
māṇ, māṇṭāṉ
---

47. teriniliviṉip pakutikaḷ, vikuti mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu puṇarumpōtu, iyalpākiyum,
vikāramākiyum varum.
utāraṇam.
1. toḻu: toḻutāṉ iyalapāki vantaṉa uṇ, uṇṭāṉ
2. cēṟal: iṅkē colleṉpakuti mutal nīṇṭatu.
tantāṉ: iṅkē tāveṉpakuti mutal kuṟakiyatu.
tarukiṉṟāṉ: iṅkē tāveṉpakuti mutal kuṟuki, rukaravuyirmey viriyappeṟṟatu.
cettāṉ: iṅkē cāveṉpakuti mutalākaram ekarāmāyt tirintatu.
virāviṉāṉ: iṅkē virāveṉpakuti naṭukkuṟil nīṇṭatu.
koṇārntāṉ: iṅkē koṇāveṉ pakutiyīṟṟu neṭil kuṟiki, rakaramey virintatu.
kaṟṟāṉ: iṅkē kalleṉ pakutiyīṟṟu mey varumeḻuttāyt tirintatu.
ceṉṟāṉ: iṅkē colleṉ pakutiyīṟṟu mey varumeḻuttukku iṉamāyt tirintatu.
----

48. torinili viṉippakutikaḷ, vi, pi, mutaliya vikuti peṟṟēṉum,
vikārappaṭṭēṉum, vikārappaṭṭu vikuti peṟṟēṉum, piṟaviṉip pakutikaḷāy varum.
utāraṇam.
1. cey, ceyvi, ceyvittāṉ naṭa, naṭappi, naṭappittāṉ
2. tiruntu, tiruttu, tiruttiṉāṉ āṭu, āṭṭu, āṭṭiṉāṉ tēṟu, tēṟṟu, tēṟṟiṉāṉ
uruku, urukku, urukkiṉāṉ
3. tiruttu, tiruttuvi, tiruttuvittāṉ āṭṭu, āṭṭuvi, āṭṭuvittāṉ tēṟṟu,
tēṟṟuvi, tēṟṟivittāṉ urukku, urukkuvi, urukkuvittāṉ
----

49. poṉṉaṉ, kariyaṉ, mutalāṉavi, eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷuḷ oṉṟi yēṟkum pōtu
peyarppakupataṅkaḷām: mukkālaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kuṟippākak kāṭṭumpōtu viṉikkuṟippu
muṟṟuppakupataṅkaḷām: kālaṅkāṭṭutalōṭu vēṟṟumiyēṟkum pōtu kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum
peyarppakupataṅkaḷām. iviyē im mūṉṟukkum vēṟupāṭam.
---

50. naṭantāṉ, vantāṉ, mutalāṉavi kālaṅkāṭṭum pōtu terinili viṉi muṟṟap
pakupataṅkaḷām: kālaṅkāṭṭutalōṭu vēṟṟumiyēṟkumpōtu terinili viṉiyālaṇiyum
peyarppakupataṅkaḷām. iviyē ivviraṇṭukkum vēṟupāṭām.2

60. i, ai, am eṉṉum mūṉṟu vikutikaḷum, viṉimutaṟporuḷiyuñ ceyaṟpaṭu poruḷiyum

2. ūruṇi, toṭi, tēṭṭam, eṉpaṉa ceyaṟpaṭu poruḷi uṇarttiṉa. ivi muṟiyē, ūrāluṇṇap
paṭuvatu, toṭukkappaṭuvatu, tēṭapapaṭuvatu eeṉapporuḷpaṭum.
3. maṇveṭṭi, pārvi, nōkkam, eṉpaṉa, karuvipporuḷi, uṇarttiṉa. ivi muṟiyē,
ṭaṇveṭṭaṟkaruvi, pārttaṟ karuvi, nōkkaṟkaruvi eṉap poruḷpaṭum.

61. ituvariyuṅ kūṟiya vikutikaḷēyaṉṟip piṟa vikutikaḷum uṇṭu. avi varumāṟu:-

mi vikuti taṉmipporuḷ uṇarttum.

poṉmi, āṇmi
iru, iṭu, eṉpaṉa, tamakkeṉa vēṟuporuḷ iṉṟip pakutipporuḷ iṉṟip pakutipporuḷ
vikutiyāy varum.
utāraṇam. eḻucantirukkiṉṟāṉ, urittiṭukkiṉṟāṉ.

puṇarntu keṭum vikuti
62. muṉṉiliyēvalorumi āy vikutiyum, peyerecca vikutiyum, toḻiṟpeyar
vikutiyum, viṉimutaṟ poruḷi uṇarttum ai vikutiyum, pakutiyōṭu puṇarntu niṉṟāṟ
pōlavē tamporuḷi uṇarttum.
utāraṇam. nī, naṭa, nī naṭappi: ivikaḷilē āy vikuti puṇarntu keṭṭatu.
kolkaḷiṟu, oṭākkutiri: ivikaḷilē peyarecca vikutikaḷ puṇarntu keṭṭaṉa.
aṭi, kēṭu, iṭiyīṭu : ivikaḷilē talleṉṉun toḻiṟpeyar vikuti puṇarntu keṭṭatu.
kāy, taḷir, pū, kaṉi : ivikaḷilē viṉimutaṟ poruḷi uṇarttum ikaravikuti puṇarntu
keṭṭatu.
ūṇ, tīṉ, eḻuttu : ivikaḷilē ceyappaṭu poruḷi uṇarttum ai vikuti puṇarntu
keṭṭatu.

iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.

kuṟintum varum. pēyātu eṉa yakaramey iṟantakālaviṭiniliyāyum varum.

kālaṅkāṭṭum vikuti
70. cila vikutikaḷ, iṭiniliyēlātu, tāmē kālaṅkāṭṭum, avi varumāṟu:-
tu, tum, ṟu, ṟum eṉṉum vikutikaḷ iṟantakālamum, etirkālamuṅkāṭṭum.

varum.
ku, kum eṉṉum vikutikaḷ etirkālaṅ kāṭṭum.
utāraṇam.
uṇku, (-uṇpēṉ) uṇkum, (-uṇpēm) eṉa varum
ṭu, ṭum eṉṉum vikutikaḷ iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭum.
utāraṇam.
uṇṭu, (-uṇṭēṉ) uṇṭum, (-uṇṭēm) eṉa varum.
i eṉṉum muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu vikuti yoṉṟum, pa, mar, eṉṉum paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟu
vikutiyiraṇṭum, ka, iya, iyar, al, eṉṉum viyaṅkōṇ muṟṟu vikutup nāṉkum, āy,
i, āl, ēl, kāṇ, miṉ, um, īr, eṉṉum muṉṉiliyēvaṉmuṟṟu vikutiyēḻum, ākiya
patināṉku vikutikaḷum etirkālaṅkāṭṭum.
utāraṇam.
(1). cēṟi, (-celvāy) (2) naṭappa, (-naṭappār) naṭamār, (-naṭappār) (3) vāḻka,
vāḻiya, vāḻiyar, uṇṇal (4) naṭavāy, uṇṇuti, māṟal, aḻēl, collikkāṇ, naṭamiṉ,
uṇṇum, uṇṇīr.
um
eṉṉañ ceyyumaṉ muṟṟu vikuti nikaḻkāhlamum etirkālamuṅ kāṭṭum.
utāraṇam.
uṇṇum

vikāram
74. vikāraṅkaḷāvaṉa, melliṉa meyyi valliṉa meyyākkalum, kuṟṟeḻutti
neṭṭeḻuttākkaḷum, neṭṭeḻutti kaṟṟeḻuttākkalum, illāta eḻutti virittalum, uḷḷa
eḻutti tokuttalum ām.

pataviyal muṟṟiṟṟu

------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. 3. puṇariyal

75. puṇarcciyāvatu, nilimeḻiyum varumoḻiyum oṉṭupaṭappuṇarvatām.
---

76. appuṇarcci, vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyum, al vaḻippuṇarcciyum eṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.
---

77. vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyāvatu, ai, āl, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ, eṉṉum āṟuruppukaḷum
iṭiyil maṟintāyiṉum veḷippaṭṭāyiṉum varaccokaḷ puṇarvatām.
utāraṇam.
vēṟṟumittoki vēṟṟumiviri
maramveṭṭiṉāṉ .. jaisa maratti veṭṭiṉāṉ
kalleṟintāṉ .. jālsa kallāleṟintāṉ
koṟṟaṉmakaṉ .. jakusa koṟṟaṉukku makaṉ
malivīḻaruvi .. jiṉsa maliyiṉ vīḻaruvi
cāttāṉki .. jaatusa cāttaṉatuki
malinel .. jakaṇsa maliyiṉkaṇel
---

78. alavaḻippuṇarcciyāvatu, vēṟṟumiyallāta vaḻiyiṟ puṇarvatām. ātu, viṉittoki,
paṇputtoki, uvamittoki, ummittoki, aṉmoḻittoki, eṉṉum aintu
tokinilittoṭarum, eḻuvāyttoṭar, viḷittoṭar, terinili viṉimuṟṟut toṭar,
kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuttoṭar, peyareccattoṭar, viṉiyeccattoṭar, iṭiccoṟtoṭar,
uriccoṟṟoṭar, aṭukkuttoṭar, eṉṉum oṉpatu tokānilit toṭarumākap, patiṉāṉku
vakippaṭum.
tokinilit toṭarkaḷukku utāraṇam
(1) kolyāṉi .. viṉittoki
(2) karuṅkutiri .. paṇputtoki cārippāmpu .. irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput toki
(3) matimukam .. uvamit toki
(4) irāppakal .. ummit toki
(5) poṟṟoṭi .. aṉmoḻit toki
tokānilit toṭarkaḷukku utāraṇam
(1) cāttaṉ vantāṉ .. eḻuvāyt toṭar
(2) cāttavā .. viḷit toṭar
(3) vantāṉ cāttaṉ .. torinili viṉimuṟṟut toṭar
(4) poṉṉaṉivaṉ .. kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuttoṭar
(5) vanta cāttaṉ .. peyareccat toṭar
(6) vantu pōṉāṉ .. viṉiyeccat toṭar
(7) maṟṟoṉṟu .. iṭiccoṟṟoṭar
(8) naṉipēti ..uriccoṟṟoṭar
(9) pāmpu pāmpu .. aṭukkut toṭar
---

79. ippaṭi moḻikaḷ, vēṟṟumi vaḻiyālum, alvaḻiyālum, puṇarumiṭattu,
iyalpākavāyiṉum, vikāramākavāyiṉum puṇarum.
---

80. iyalapu puṇarcciyāvatu, nilimoḻiyum, varumoḻiyum, vikāramiṉṟip puṇarvatām.
utāraṇam.
poṉmaṇi cāttaṉki
---

81. vikārappuṇarcciyāvatu, nilimoḻiyēṉum, varumoḻiyēṉum, ivviru moḻimēṉum,
tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉṉum mūṉṟu vikāraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟiyāyiṉum peṟṟup
puṇarvatām.
utāraṇam.
vāḻi + paḻam - vāḻippaḻam tōṉṟal
maṇ + kuṭam - maṭkuṭam tirital
maram + vēr - maravēr keṭutal
nilam + paṉi - nilappaṉi keṭutal, tōṉṟal
paṉi + kāy - paṉaṅkāy keṭutal, tōṉṟal, tirital
---

82. tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal, eṉṉum iv vikāramūṉṟum, mayakka viti iṉmi paṟṟiyum,
alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruṇōkkam paṟṟiyum varum.

---

mayaṅkā eḻuttukkaḷ
83. uyirōṭu uyirkku mayakkaviti iṉmiyāl, uyihPṟṟiṉmuṉ, uyir variṉ, iṭiyē
uṭampaṭu meyyeṉa oṉṟu tōṉṟum.
uṭampaṭu meyyāvatu, vanta uyirukku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey, nilimoḻiyīṟṟiṉum
varumoḻi mutaliṉum niṉṟa uyirkaḷi uṭampaṭuttum meyyeṉiṉum poruntum.
uṭampaṭuttaleṉiṉum, uṭaṉ paṭuttaleṉiṉum okkum.
---

84. meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ mayaṅkutaṟku uriyatallāta meyvariṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟēṉum, varumoḻi
mutalēṉum, ivviraṇṭumēṉum vikārappaṭum.
---

85.moḻikku īrākumeṉappaṭṭa patikorumeykaḷiṉ muṉṉum, moḻikku mutalākumeṉappaṭṭa
oṉpatu meykaḷum puṇarumpōtu, mayaṅkutaṟku uriyaṉavallāta meykaḷic colvām :-
lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉē ta ña na ma eṉṉum nāṉkum mayaṅkā. ṇukara ṉakaraṅkaḷaḻaṉ muṉṉē ta na
eṉṉum iraṇṭum mayaṅkā. mukara meyyiṉ muṉṉē ka ca ta ña na eṉṉum iraṇṭum mayaṅkā
ñakarattiṉ muṉṉē cakaramum yakaramumallāta ēḻam mayaṅkā. nukarattiṉ muṉṉē takaramum
yakaramumallāta ēḻam mayaṅkā. vukarattiṉ muṉṉē yakaramallāta eṭṭum mayaṅkā.

---

meyyīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyir puṇartal

mēl ēṟum.

kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉ uyirum yakaramum puṇartal

cila muṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirum yakaramum puṇartal

107. i, y, ā, ū, eṉa, a eṉṉum vikutikaḷiyuṭiya terinili viṉi viṉiyeccaṅkaḷiṉ

nakaramun takaramum variṉ, ainnūṟu, aintūṇi, eṉa īṟṟuyir mey māttiraṅ keṭum.
āṟeṉṉum eṇ uyir variṟ potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyum: meyvariṉ mutal kuṟukum.
utāraṇam.
āṟu + āyiram - āṟāyiram
kaḻañcu - aṟukaḻañcu
maṇi - aṟumaṇi
vaḻi - aṟuvaḻi
ēḻu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ muṉ uyir variṉ, īṟṟukaraṅ keṭum: meyvariṉ mutal kuṟukum.
utāraṇam.
ēḻu + āyiram - ēḻāyiram
kaḻañcu - eḻukaḻañcu
maṇi - eḻumaṇi
vaki - eḻuvaki
ēḻkaṭal, ēḻpari eṉa varutalumuṇṭu.
eṭaṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ īṟṟuyir mey keṭum: niṉṟa ṭakaramey nāṟkaṇattiṉ muṉṉum
ṇakarameyyākattiriyum.
utāraṇam.
eṭaṭu + āyiram - eṇṇāyiram
kaḻañcu - eṇkaḻañcu
maṇi - eṇmaṇi
vaḷi - eṇvaḷi
iv vikāraṅkaḷiṉṟip potu viti paṟṟi, iraṇṭu kaḻañcu, mūṉṟu paṭi, nāṉku poruḷ,
aintu mukam, āṟu kuṇam, ēḻu kaṭal, eṭṭut tikku eṉavum varumeṉak koḷka.
---

131. oṉpateṉṉum eṇmuṉ patteṉṉum eṇvariṉ, patu keṭṭu, mutaluyirōṭu takaramey
cērntu, niṉṟa ṉakaram ḷakaramākavum. varumoḻiyākiya nūṟu āyiramāvun tiriyum.
utāraṇam.
oṉapatu + pattu - toṇṇuhṟu
oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇmuṉ nūṟu eṉṉum eṇ variṉ, patu keṭṭu, mutaluyirōṭu
takarameycērntu, niṉṟa ṉakaram ḷakaramākavum, varumoḻiyākiya nūṟu āyiramākavun tiriyum.
utāraṇam.
eṉpatu + nūṟu - toḷḷāyiram
itu ikkālattut toḷāyiram eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
---

132. oṉṟu mutal eṭaṭīṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyar kaṇmuṉ patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyar variṉ,
appattiṉ naṭu niṉṟa takaramey, keṭṭāyiṉum, īytamākat tirintāyiṉum puṇarum.

133. orupatu mutal eṇpatu īṟākiya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpateṇṇum avaṟṟiyaṭutta
piṟa peyarum variṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟukku ayalilē takaravoṟṟut tōṉṟum.

meyyīṟṟu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉum ēval viṉi muṉṉum mey puṇartal
137. ñ, ṇ, n, m, l, v, ḷ, ṉ eṉṉum ivveṭṭu meyyīṟṟu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarum,

toḻippeyarp poruḷit taruvatām.

ṇa kara ṉakara vīṟṟup puṇarcci
138. ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ valliṉam variṉ, al vaḻiyil avviru meykaḷum iyalapām.
vēṟṟumiyil ṇakaram ṭakaramākavum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākavun tiriyum. avviru vaḻiyilum,
varuntakaram ṇakarattiṉ muṉ ṭakaramākavum, ṉakarattiṉ muṉ ṟakaramākavum tiriyum.
utāraṇam.
alvaḻi vēṟṟumi
maṇciṟitu maṭcāṭi
maṇṭītu maṭṭūṇ
poṉ kuṟitu poṟkalam
poṉṟītu poṟṟūṇ
kaṭpoṟi, poṟkōṭu eṉap paṇput tokiyiṉum paṭcol, poṟcuṇaṅku eṉa uvamit tokiyiṉun
tiritalum uṇṭu.
maṇ cumantāṉ, poṉ koṭuttāṉ, eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyiṉum, viṇ paṟantatu, kāṉ
pukuntāṉ eṉa ēḻām vēṟṟumit tokiyiṉum, varumoḻi viṉiyāyaviṭattut
tiriyātiyalpām. maṇkūṭi, puṇki, eṉa orōviṭattu iraṇṭaṉurupum payaṉum uṭaṉ ṟokka
tokiyiṉun tiriyāmi koḷka.
----

139. ṇakara, ṉakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ melliṉamum iṭiyiṉamum variṉ, iṟuti ṇa ṉakkaḷ iru
vaḻiyiṉum iyalpām. avviru vaḻiyilum, ṇakarattiṉ muṉ varu nakaram ṇakaramākavum:
ṉakarattiṉ muṉ varu nakaram ṉakaramākavun tiriyum.

makaravīṟṟup puṇarcci

lakara ḷakara vīṟṟup puṇarcci
149. lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷil muṉ valliṉam variṉ, vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilē paṇput
tokiyilum, uvamit tokiyilum, iṟuti lakaram ṟakaramākavum ḷakaram ṭakaramākavun tiriyum.
eḻuvāyt toṭariṉulum ummit tokiyilun tiriyātiyalpām.

kuyilkaritu poruḷ peritu - eḻuvāy
kālki poruḷpukaḻ - ummittoki
pāl kuṭittāṉ, aruḷpeṟṟāṉ eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tokiyiṉum, kālkutittōṭiṉāṉ,
vālpōḻntiṭṭāṉ eṉa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tokiyiṉum, varumoḻi viṉiyāyaviṭattu
tiriyāveṉakkoḷka.
----

150. taṉikkuṟṟeḻuttic cārnta la ḷa kkaḷ, valliṉam variṉ, eḻuvāyt toṭarilum,
ummitokiyilum, oru kāl iyalpākavum, oru kāṟtiriyavum peṟum.

coṟperitu eṉa eḻuvāytterṭarilum uṟaḻātu tiritē varum.

uṟaḻcciyāvatu oru kāl iyalpākiyum, oru kāl vikārappaṭṭum varutal. uṟāḻcci
eṉiṉum, vikaṟppa viṉumeṉiṉum ōṅkum.
kaṟkarittāṉ, kaṭkuṭittāṉ eṉattaṉik kūṟṟeḻutti cārnh la ḷa kkaḷ, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit
tokiyiṉ varumoḻi viṉiyāyaviṭattum, iyalpākātu. tirintē niṟkum.
----

151 alvaḻi vēṟṟumi iraṇṭiṉum lakarattiṉ muṉ varun takaram ṟakaramākavum, ḷakarattiṉ muṉ
varun takaram ṭakaramākavun tiriyum.

urupu puṇarcci
----

164. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ, nilimoḻiyōṭum varumoḻiyōṭum puṇarumiṭattu, avvurupiṉ
poruṭpuṇarccikku muṟ kūṟiya vitikaḷip perumpāṉmi peṟum: ciṟupāṉmi vēṟupaṭṭum
varum.
utāraṇam.
nampikkaṇ vāḻvu: iṅkē uyartiṇip peyāPṟṟu uyirmuṉ vēṟṟumip poruḷil varum
valliṉamikā, eṉṟum, ṇakaram iṭiyiṉam variṉ iṟuvaḻiyiṉum iyalpām, eṉṟum,
vitittapaṭiyē, kaṇṇurupiṉ mutalumīṟum iyalpāyiṉa.
uṟikkaṭṭayir: iṅkē ikaravīṟṟaḵṟiṇip peyarmuṉ vēṟṟumip poruḷil varum valliṉam mikum
eṉṟum, ṇakaram vēṟṟumip poruḷil valliṉam variṉ, ṭakaramākat tiriyum eṉṟum,
vitittapaṭiyē, kaṇṇurupiṉ mutalumīṟum vikāramāyiṉa.
nampikkup piḷḷi: iṅkē uyartiṇip peyāPṟṟu uyir muṉ vēṟṟumi poruḷil varum
valliṉam iyalpām eṉṟu vitittapaṭi iyalpākātu kuvvurupu mikuntatu.
iṅṅaṉam urupu puṇarcciyāṉatu poruṭpuṇarcciyi ottu varutalum, atiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu
varutalum, atiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu varutalum, cāṉṟōrāṭciyāl aṟintu koḷka.
uṟittayir eṉpatu, kaṇṇeṉṉum ēḻām vēṟṟumiyurupiṉṟi avvurupiṉatu iṭapporuḷ
paṭappeyarum nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāy niṉṟu puṇarnta puṇarcci yātaliṉ,
poruṭpuṇarcciyeṉappaṭṭatu. uṟikkaṭṭayir eṉpatu, avvēḻaṉurupu veḷippaṭaṭṭu niṉṟu
nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyātaliṉ, urupu puṇarcciyeṉappaṭṭatu.

taṉimoḻiyilum varum.

iraṇṭāvatu: collatikāram

2.1. peyariyal

173. collāvatu, oruvar taṅkaruttiṉ nikaḻporuḷip piṟārkku aṟivittaṟkum, piṟar
karuttiṉ nikaḻ poruḷit tām aṟitaṟkuṅ karuviyākiya oliyām.

---

tiṇi
174. akkaruttiṉ nikaḻporuḷ, uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉa, iru vakippaṭum.
tiṇi- cāti, uyartaṇi - uyarvākiya cāti, aḵṟiṇi - uyarvallāta cāti. altiṇi eṉṟatu
aḵṟiṇi eṉap puṇarntatu. iṅkē tiṇi eṉṉum paṇpup peyar, āku peyarāyp paṇpiyi
uṇartti niṉṟatu. cāti paṇpu, cātiyiyuṭiya poruḷ paṇpi.
---

175. uyartaṇiyāvaṉa, maṉitarum, tēvarum, narakarum ākiya mūvakic cātip poruḷkaḷām.
---

176. aḵṟiṇiyāvaṉa, mirukam, paṟavi mutaliya uyiruḷḷa cātip poruḷkaḷum, nilam, nīr,
mutaliya uyirallāta cātip poruḷkaḷumām.

----

pāl
177. uyartiṇi, āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, eṉa mūṉṟu pirivuṭiyatu.
utāraṇam.
avaṉ, vantāṉ - uyartiṇiyāṇpāl
avaḷ, vantāḷ - uyartiṇip peṇpāl
avar, vantār - uyartiṇip palarpāl
palarpāl eṉṟatu, āṭavar, kāḷiyar eṉpaṉa mutaliya āṇ paṉmiyum, peṇṭīr, maṅkiyar mutaliya
peṇpaṉmiyum, makkaḷ, avar eṉpaṉa mutaliya avviruvar paṉmiyum, aṭakki niṉṟatu.
---

178. aḵṟiṇi, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa, iraṇṭu piriviyuṭiyatu.
utāraṇam.
atu, vantatu - aḵṟiṇiyoṉṟaṉpāl
avi, vantaṉa - aḵṟiṇip palarpāl

---

iṭam
179. ivviru tiṇiyākiya aimpāṟporuḷi uṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ, taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkki,
eṉṉum mūviṭattiyum paṟṟi varum.
---

180. pēcum poruḷ taṉmiyiṭam: pēcum poruḷiṉāl etirmukamākkappaṭṭuk kēṭkum
poruḷ muṉṉiliyiṭam: pēcappaṭum poruḷ paṭarkkiyiṭam.

----

coṟkaḷiṉ vaki
181. coṟkaḷ, peyarccol viṉiccol, iṭiccol, uriccol eṉa nālvakippaṭum.

---

peyarc coṟkaḷiṉ vaki
182. peyarc collāvatu, poṟikaṭkum maṉattukkum viṭayamākiya poruḷi uyarttum.
poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi eṉṉam nāṉkum nhruḷeṉa uṉṟāy aṭaṅkum. poruṭku urimi
pūṇṭu niṟpaṉavākiya paṇput toḻilum poruḷeṉavum paṭumātaliṉ, avikaḷi uyarttuñ
collum peyarc colleṉappaṭum. poruḷiṉatu puṭip peyarcci yeṉappaṭum viṉi
nikaḻcciyi uṇarttuñ collākiya viṉic collum, peyarttaṉmippaṭṭu, apporuḷi
uṇarttum. iṅṅaṉamākavē, peyarkaḷaṉittum, poraṭpeyar, viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar,
paṇpuppeyar, toḻiṟpeyar eṉṉum nālvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
-----

183. peyarc coṟkaḷ, iṭukuṟip peyar, kāraṇappeyar, kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyar eṉa,
mūvakippaṭum.
-----

184. iṭukuṟip peyarāvatu, oru kāraṇamum paṟṟātu poruḷi uṇartti niṟkum peyarām.
utāraṇam. maram, mali, kaṭal, cōṟu
ivi oru kāraṇamum paṟṟātu vantamiyāl, iṭu kaṟip peyarāyiṉa.
-----

185. kāraṇappeyarāvatu, yātēṉum oru kāraṇam paṟṟip poruḷi uṇartti niṟkum peyarām.
utāraṇam.
paṟavi, aṇi, poṉṉaṉ, kaṇakkaṉ
paṟappatātaliṟ paṟavi eṉavum, aṇiyappaṭuvatātaliṉ aṇi eṉavum, poṉṉiyuṭiyaṉātaliṟ poṉṉaṉ
eṉavum, kaṇakkeḻutuvōṉātaliṟ kaṇakkaṉ eṉavum kāraṇam paṟṟi vantamiyāl, ivi kāraṇap
peyarāyiṉa.
-----

186. kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyarāvatu, kāraṇaṅ karutiya poḻutu akkāraṇattiyuṭiya pala
poruḷkaḷukkuñ celvatāyum, kāraṇaṅ karutāta poḻutu iṭukuṟikaḷavāy niṉṟu ovvoru
poruṭkē celvatāyum, uḷḷa peyarām.
utāraṇam.
mukkaṇaṉ, antaṉaṉ, muḷḷi, kaṟaṅku
mukkaṇaṉ eṉpatu, kāraṇaṅ karutiya poḻutu vināyakak kaṭavuḷ mutaliya palarkkuñ
cellutalālum, kāraṇaṅkarutāta poḻutu iṭukuṟiyāḷavāyc civaperumāṉukkuc
cellutalālum, kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyarāyiṟṟu.
antaṇaṉ eṉpatu, kāraṇaṅkarutiya poḻutu aḻakiya taṇṇaḷiyiyuṭiyayōr palarkkuñ
cellutalālum, kāraṇaṅkarutāta pōtu iṭukuṟiyāḷavāyp pārppāṉukkuc cellutalālum,
kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyarāyiṟṟu.
muḷḷi eṉpatu, kāraṇaṅkarutiya poḻutu muḷḷiyuṭiya ceṭikaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkuñ
cellutalālum, kāraṇaṅkarutāta pōtu iṭukuṟiyāḷavāy muḷḷi eṉṉum oru ceṭikku
cellutalālum, kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyarāyiṟṟu.
kaṟaṅku eṉpatu, kāraṇaṅkarutiya poḻutu cuḻaliyuṭi pala poruḷkaṭkuñ cellutalālum,
kāraṇaṅkarutāta pōtu iṭukuṟiyāḷavāyk kāṟṟāṭi eṉṉum oru poruṭkuc cellutalālum,
kāraṇaviṭukuṟip peyarāyiṟṟu.
-----

187. ippeyarkaḷ, potuppeyar, ciṟappupnpeyar eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
-----

188. potuppeyarāvatu, pala poruḷkaḷukkup potuvāki varum peyarām
utāraṇam.
maram, vilaṅku, paṟavi
ivaṟṟuḷ, maram iṭukuṟip potuppeyar: vilaṅku paṟavi eṉpaṉa kāraṇappotup peyar.
-----

189. ciṟappup peyarāvatu, ovvoru poruḷukkē ciṟappāki varum peyarām.
utāraṇam.
āl, kari, kāri
ivaṟṟul āl iṭu kuṟip peyar: kari, kāri eṉpaṉa kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar. kari- yāṉi,
kāri - karikkuruvi.
-----

190. peyarkaḷ, iṭavēṟṟumiyiṉālē, taṉmip peyar, muṉṉilip peyar, paṭarkkip peyar,
eṉa mūvakippaṭum.

---

taṉmippeyarkaḷ
191. taṉmippeyarkaḷ, nāṉ, yāṉ, nām, yām, eṉa nāṉkām. ivikaḷuḷ nāṉ, yāṉ
ivviraṇṭum orumippeyarkaḷ: nām, yām ivviraṇṭum paṉmip peyarkaḷ.
ittaṉmip peyarkaḷ uyartiṇiyāṇpāl peṇpālkaḷukkup potuvāki varuvaṉavākum.
utāraṇam.
yāṉampi, yāṉaṅki - taṉmiyorumi
yāmintar, yāmakaḷir - taṉmip paṉmi
ulaka vaḻakkuc ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiraṇṭiṉum, nām, yām, iraṇṭum. nāṅkaḷ, yāṅkaḷ eṉavum
varum.

----

muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ
192. muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ, nī, nīr, nīyir, nīvir, ellīr eṉa aintām. ivikaḷuḷ, nī eṉpatu
orumip peyar: maṟṟavi paṉmip peyarkaḷ.
utāraṇam.
nī nampi, nī naṅki, nī pūtam
- muṉṉiliyorumi
nīr mintar, nīr makaḷir, nīr pa10taṅkaḷ
- muṉṉilippaṉmi
w taṉmip peyarkaḷi uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ eṉpar tolkāppiyar; iru tiyip potup peyarkaḷ
eṉpar naṉṉūlār.
iru vaḻakkiṉum nīṅkaḷ eṉpatum muṉṉilip paṉmiyil varum.

---

paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ
193. paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, mēṟcollappaṭṭa taṉmi muṉṉilip peyarkaḷallāta maṟṟiya ellāp
peyarkaḷumām.
utāraṇam.
avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, poṉ, maṇi, nilam
---

194. aṉ. āḷ, i, ḷ eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uyuṭiya poyarkaḷ
uyartiṇiyāṇpālorumip paṭarkkip peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
poṉṉaṉ, poruḷāṉ, vaṭamaṉ, kōmaṉ, piṟaṉ
-----

195. aḷ, āḷ, kaḷ, mār, r, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uyuṭiya poyarkaḷ uyartiṇip
peṇpālorumip paṭarkkip peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
kuḻiyaḷ, kuḻiyāḷ, poṉṉi, piṟaḷ
-----

196. ar, ār, kaḷ, mār, r, eṉṉutam vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uyuṭiya poyarkaḷ
uyartiṇip palarpāṟ paṭarkkip peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
kuḻiyar, kuḻiyār, kōkkaḷ, tēvimār, piṟar
taccarkaḷ, taṭṭārkaḷ, eṉak kaḷ viputi, vikutimēl vikutiyāyum varum.
-----

197. tuv vikutiyi iṟutiyil uṭiya peyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇiyoṉṟaṉ pāṟ paṭarkkip peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
kuḻiyatu
---

198. vi, a, kaḷ, v, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya peyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇip palaviṉpāṟ
paṭarkkip peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
kuḻiyavi, kuḻiyaṉa, maraṅkaḷ, av.
---

199. vikuti peṟātu uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇikaḷil āṇpāl peṇpālkaḷi uṇartti varum
peyarkaḷuñ cila uṇṭu. avi varumāṟu:-
nampi, viṭali, kō, vēḷ, āṭūu, mutaliyaṉa uyartiṇiyāṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ.
mātu, tiyal, makaṭūu, naṅki mutaliyaṉa uyartiṇip peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ.
kaṭuvaṉ, oruttal, pōttu, kali, cēval, ēṟu mutaliyaṉa aḵṟiṇiyāṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ
piṭi, piṇa, peṭṭi, manti, piṇā mutaliyaṉa peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ.

-----

irutiṇip potup peyar
200. tanti, tāy; cāttāṉ. cātti; koṟṟaṉ, koṟṟi; āṇ, peṇ; ceviyili,
ceviyilikaḷ; tāṉ, tām eṉa varum paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi iraṇṭaṟkum
potup peyarkaḷām. potup peyareṉiṉum, poruntum.
utāraṇam.
tantiyivaṉ
tantiyivverutu tantiyeṉpatu irutiṇi yāṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
tāyivaḷ
tāyippacu tāyeṉpatu irutiṇi peṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
cāttaṉivaṉ
cāttaṉivverutu cāttāṉeṉpatu irutiṇi yāṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
cāttiyivaḷ
cāttiyippacu cāttiyeṉpatu irutiṇi peṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
koṟṟaṉivaṉ
koṟṟaṉivverutu koṟṟaṉeṉpatu irutiṇi yāṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
koṟṟiyivaḷ
koṟṟiyippacu koṟṟiyeṉkatu irutiṇi peṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
āṇ vantāṉ
āṇvantatu āṇeṉpatu irutiṇi yāṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
peṇ vantāḷ
peṇvantatu peṇṉeṉpatu irutiṇi peṇpāṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
ceviyiliyavaṉ
ceviyiliyivaḷ
ceviyiliyivverutu
ceviyiliyippacu ceviyili eṉpatu irutiṇi yerumikku potuvāyiṟṟu.
ceviyilikaḷivar
ceviyilikaḷivi ceviyilikaḷeṉpatu irutiṇip paṉmikku potuvāyiṟṟu.
avaṉṟāṉ
avaṭāṉ
atutāṉ tāṉeṉpatu irutiṇi yorumikkum potuvāyiṟṟu.
avartam
avitam tāmeṉpatu irutiṇip paṉmikku
potuvāyiṟṟu.
tām eṉpatu tāṅkaḷ eṉavum varum.

----

iru tiṇi mūviṭap potuppeyar
201. ellām eṉṉum paṉmip peyar iru tiṇi mūviṭaṅkaṭkum potuppeyaram.
utāraṇam.
nāmellām, nīrellām, avarellām, aviyellām.

---

uyartiṇiyiṟ pāṟ potuppeyar
202. oruvar pēti, ūmi, eṉa varum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇiyāṉ peṇṇeṉṉum iru pāṟkum
potup peyarkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
āṭavaḷoruvar peṇṭiḷoruvar
pētiyavaṉ pētiyavaḷ
ūmiyivaṉ ūmiyivaḷ
oruvar eṉṉum pāṟpotuppeyar, poruṭkēṟpa oramic collik koḷḷātu, oluvar vantār
eṉac coṟkēṟpap paṉmic colliyē koṇṭu muṭiyum. iṉṉuñ cāttaṉār, tēvaṉār eṉpaṉavum,
poruṭkēṟpa orumic collik koḷḷātu, coṟkēppap paṉmic colliyē koṇṭu muṭiyum.

-----

aḵṟiṇiyiṟ pāṟ potup peyar
203. tu eṉṉum orumivikutiyiyāyiṉum, vi, a, kaḷ eṉṉum paṉmi
vikutikaḷiyāyiṉum peṟātu varum. aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷellām, attiṇi oṉṟu pala eṉṉum
irupāṟkum potuppeyarkaḷām. ivi pālpakāḵṟiṇip peyar eṉavum, aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟ peyar
eṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭum.
utāraṇam.
yāṉi vantatu yāṉi vantaṉa
maram vaḷarntatu maram vaḷarntaṉa
kaṇ civantatu kaṇ civantaṉa

---

ākupeyar
204. oru peruḷiṉ iyaṟ peyar, apporuḷōṭu campantamuṭiya piṟitoru poruḷukkut
toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki variṉ, atu āku peyareṉappaṭum.
-----

205. avvāku peyar, patiṉā vakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa:- poruḷāku peyar,
iṭavāku peyar, kālavāku peyar, ciṉiyāku peyar, kuṇavāku peyar, toḻilāku peyar,
eṇṇalaḷaviyāku peyar, eṭuttaḷaviyāku peyar, mukattaḷaviyāku peyar, nīṭṭalaḷaviyāku
peyar, collāku peyar, taṉiyāku peyar, karuviyāku peyar, kāriyavāku peyar,
karuttāvāku peyar, uvamiyāku peyar eṉpaṉavām.
utāraṇam.
(1). tāmari pōlumukam: iṅkē tāmariyeṉṉu mutaṟporuḷiṉ peyar ataṉ ciṉiyākiya
malarukkātalāṟ poruḷāku peyar.
(2). ūraṭaṅkiṟṟu: iṅkē ūreṉṉupamiṭappeyar aṅkirukkiṟa maṉitarukkātalāl iṭavāku
peyar.
(3). kāraṟuttatu: iṅkē kāreṉṉum maḻikkālap peyar akkālattil viḷiyum
payirukkātalāṟ kālavāku peyar
(4). veṟṟili naṭṭāṉ: iṅkē veṟṟili yeṉṉumñ ciṉippeyar ataṉ matalākiya
koṭikkatalāṟ ciṉiyāku peyar.
(5). nīlañ cūṭiṉāṉ: iṅkē nīlameṉṉum niṟakkuṇap peyar ataṉiyuṭiya kuvaḷi
malarukkātalāṟ kuṇavāku peyar.
(6). vaṟṟalōṭuṇṭāṉ: iṅkē vaṟṟaleṉṉun toḻiṟpeyar ataṉip poruntiya uṇaviṟkātalāṟ
ṟoḻilāku peyar.
(7). kālālē naṭantāṉ: iṅkē kāleṉṉum eṇṇaḷavip peyar avvaḷavik koṇṭa
uṟuppiṟkātalāl eṇṇalaviyākupeyar.
(8). iraṇṭuvīci tantāṉ: iṅkē vīci yeṉṉum eṭutta laḷavippeyar avvaḷavik koṇṭa
uṟippiṟkātalāl eṇṇalaviyāku peyar.
(9). nāḻiyuṭintatu: iṅkē nāḻiyeṉṉum mukattaḷavip peyar avvaḷavik paruvikkātalāl
mukattaḷaviyāku peyar.
(10). kīḻittaṭi viḷintatu: iṅkē taṭi yeṉṉum, nīṭṭaḷavippeyar ataṉāl aḷakkappaṭṭa
viḷinilattiṟ kātalāl nīṭṭalaḷaviyāku peyar.
(11). iṟnūṟkuri ceytāṉ: iṅkē uriyeṉṉuñ colliṉ peyar ataṉ poruḷukkātalāṟ
colāku peyar.
(12). viḷakku muṟintatu: iṅkē viḷakkeṉṉun tāṉiyiṉ peyar ataṟku tāṉamākiya
taṇṭiṟkātalāṟ nāṉiyāku peyar.
(13). tiruvācakamōtiṉāṉ: iṅkē vācakameṉṉuṅ karuvip peyar ataṉ kāriyamākiya oru
nūliṟkātalāṟ karuviyāku peyar.
(14). alaṅkāraṅkaṟṟāṉ: iṅkē alaṅkārameṉṉuṅ kāriyattiṉ peyar ataṉiyuṇarttutaṟkuk
karuviyākiya nūliṟkātalāṟ kāriyavāku peyar.
(15). tiruvaḷḷuvar paṭittāṉ: iṅkē tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉṉuṅ karuttāviṉ peyar avarāṟ
ceyyappaṭṭa nūliṟkātalāṟ karuttāvāku peyar.
(16). pāvi vantāḷ: iṅkē pāvi eṉṉum uvamiyiṉ peyar ataṉi yuvamiyākak koṇṭa
peṇṇukkātalāl uvamiyāku peyar.
kār eṉṉum karu niṟattiṉ peyar, ataṉuṭiya mēkatti yuṇarttum pōtu āku peyar: am
mēkam peyyum paruvatti uṇarttum pōtu iru maṭiyāku peyar: apparuvattil viḷiyum
neṟpayiri uṇarttum pōtu mummaṭiyāku peyar.
veṟṟili naṭṭāṉ, tiruvācakamōtiṉāṉ, eṉpavaṟṟuḷ, iliyeṉpatu veṟumiyeṉṉum
aṭiyiṉiyum, vācakameṉpatu tiruveṉṉum aṭiyiṉiyum aṭuttu, āku peyarāy varuvatāl
aṭiyaṭuttavāku peyar

-----

vēṟṟumi
206. peyarkaḷaṉittum, mutal vēṟṟumi, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi, nāṉkām
vēṟṟumi, aintām vēṟṟumi, āṟām vēṟṟumi, ēḻām vēṟṟumi, eṭṭām vēṟṟumi, eṉa
eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷi yēṟkum.
ivaṟṟuḷ mutal vēṟṟumi eḻuvāy eṉavum, peyarvēṟṟumi viḷiyeṉavum peyar peṟum.
-----

207. mutal vēṟṟumiyiṉatu urupāvatu tiripillāta peyarēyām.
itu viṉiyiyum, peyariyum, viṉāviyuṅ koḷḷum.
utāraṇam.
cāttāṉ vantāṉ, cāttaṉivaṉ, cāttaṉ yār
vēṟṟumiyurupiṉālē koḷḷappaṭuñ col, muṭikkuñ col eṉavum, payaṉili eṉavum, peyar
peṟum.
ittiripillāta peyar, tāṉē taṉ poruḷi viṉimutaṟ poruḷāka vēṟupaṭuttum. appaṭi
vēṟupaṭṭa viṉimutaṟ poruḷē itaṉ poruḷām.
viṉimutal, karuttā, ceypavaṉ eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
iv veḻuvāykku vēṟurupu illiyāyiṉum, āṉavaṉ, ākiṉṟavaṉ, āvāṉ, eṉpavaṉ, mutaliya
aimpāṟ coṟkaḷuñ ciṟupāṉmi collurupāka varum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉāṉavaṉ vantāṉ
cāttiyāṉavaṉ vantāṉ
cāttarāṉavar vantār
maramāṉatu vaḷarntatu
maṟṟavikaḷum ippaṭiyē.
-----

208. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyiṉatu urupu aiyoṉṟēyām.
itu viṉiyiyum, viṉikkuṟippiyuṅ koḷḷum.
ivviyurupu taṉṉiyēṟṟa peyarpporuḷic ceyaṟpaṭu poruḷāka vēṟupaṭuttum, appaṭi
vēṟupaṭṭa ceyaṟpaṭa poruḷē ivvurupiṉ poruḷām.
ceyappaṭuporuḷ, karumam, kāriyam, eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
acceyaṟpaṭu poruḷāṉatu, ākkappaṭuporuḷ, aḻikkappaṭu poruḷ, aṭiyappaṭa poruḷ,
okkappaṭaporuḷ, uṭamipporuḷ, mutaliyaṉavākap pala tiṟakkappaṭum.
(utāraṇam)
kuṭatti vaṉintāṉ - ākkappaṭu poruḷ
kōṭṭiyip piṭittāṉ - aḻikkappaṭu poruḷ
ūriyaṭintāṉ - aṭiyappaṭuporuḷ
maṉiviyit tuṟantāṉ - tuṟakkappaṭu poruḷ
puliyiyottāṉ - okkappaṭuporuḷ
poṉṉiyuṭiyāṉ - uṭimip poruḷ
-----

209. mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyiṉuṭi urupukaḷ āl, āṉ, oṭu, ōṭu eṉpaṉavikaḷām.
ivi viṉiyik koḷḷum.
ivvurupukaḷuḷ, āṉ, āṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭurupukaḷuma, tammiyēṟṟa peyarp poruḷik, karuviya
poruḷākavum, karutāpporuḷākavum, vēṟupaṭuttum appaṭi vēṟu paṭṭa karuvip poruḷuṅ
karutāpporuḷum ivvurupukaḷiṉ poruḷām. karuvi, kāraṇam eṉpaṉa oru poraṭ coṟkaḷ.
karuvi, mutaṟkaruvi, tuṇikkaruvi, eṉa iruvakippaṭum. karuttāvum, iyaṟṟutaṟkarattā,
ēvutaṟ karuttā eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
oṭu, ōṭu eṉṉum iraṇṭurupukaḷum, tammiyēṟṟa peyarpporuḷi uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷāka
vēṟupaṭuttum. appaṭi vēṟupaṭṭa uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷē ivvurupu kaḷiṉ perruḷām.
(utāraṇam)
maṇṇālākiya kuṭam
maṇṇaṉākiya kuṭam mutaṟkaruvi
tirikiyālākiya kuṭam
tirikiyāṉākiya kuṭam tuṇikkaruvi
taccaṉālākiya kōyil
taccaṉāṉākiya kōyil iyaṟṟutaṟkaruttā
aracaṉālākiya kōyil
aracaṉāṉākiya kōyil ēvutaṟkaruttā
makaṉōṭu tanti vantāṉ

ivvurupukaḷuḷ, āl, āṉ urupukaḷ niṟṟaṟkuriya viṭattuk koṇṭeṉpatum, ōṭu, oṭu

210. nāṉkām veṟṟumiyiṉatu urupu kuvvoṉṟēyām.
itu viṉiyiyum viṉiyōṭu poruntum peyariyuṅ koḷḷum.
ikkuvvurupu, taṉṉiyēṟṟa peyarp poruḷik kōṭaṟporuḷākavum, pakitoṭar poruḷākavum,

nimitta kāraṇa kāriyapporuḷākavum, muṟikkiyi poruḷākavum. vēṟupaṭuttum. appaṭi
vēṟu paṭṭa koṭaṟ poruṇ mutaliyaṉa ivvurupiṉ poruḷkaḷām.

kuvvurupiṉmēl ākaveṉpatuñ collurupukaḷāka varum.

212. āṟām vēṟṟumiyiṉuṭi urupukaḷ atu, ātu, ē eṉpaṉavām.
ivikaḷuḷ, atu, ātu urupukaḷ aḵṟiṇi yorumip peyariyum, a urupu aḵṟiṇip paṉmip
peyariyuṅ koḷḷum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉatu ki, taṉatu ki, taṉa kikaḷ
ivvulupukaḷ, tammiyēṟṟa peyarpporuḷi varumoḻip peyarāki taṟkiḻamip poruḷōṭum
piṟitiṉ kiḻamip poruḷōṭuñ campanta muṭiya poruḷāka, vēṟupaṭuttum. appaṭi vēṟupaṭṭa
campantapporuḷē ivvurupukaḷiṉ poruḷām.
taṟkiḻamip poruḷāvatu, taṉṉōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiya poruḷ. atu, uṟuppum, paṇpum,
toḻilum, oṉṟaṉ kūṭṭamum, palaviṉ kūṭṭamum, oṉṟu tirintoṉṟāyatum eṉa, āṟ
vakippaṭum.
(utāraṇam)
cāttāṉatu ki - uṟupputtaṟkiḻami
cāttaṉatu karumi - paṇputaṟkiḻami
cāttaṉatu varavu - toḻiṟṟaṟkiḻami
nellatu kuppi - oṉṟaṉkūṭṭaṟtaṟkiḻami
cēṉiyatu tokuti - palaviṉkūṭṭattaṟkiḻami
mañcaḷatu poṭi - oṉṟu tirintoṉṟāyataṉ taṟkiḻami
piṟitiṉkiḻamip poruḷāvatu, taṉṉiṉ vēṟāy poruḷ. atu, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, eṉa
mūvakippaṭum.
(utāraṇam)
murukaṉatu vēl - poruṭpiṟitiṉ kiḻami
murukaṉatu mali - iṭappiṟitiṉkiḻami
māraṉatu vēṉil - kālappiṟitiṉ kiḻami
ivvurupukaḷ niṟṟaṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷil, uṭiya eṉpatu collurupāka vantu, iru
tiṇiyorumi paṉmip peyariyuṅ koḷḷum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉuṭiya putalvaṉ, cāttaṉuṭiya putalvar
cāttaṉuṭiya vīṭu, cāttaṉuṭiya vīṭukaḷ

peyarkaḷiyuṅ koḷḷumeṉa vaṟika.
ivvīṭāṉatu, attōṭṭamavaṉatu eṉa vavuruvaṉa vaṟṟil, eṉatu, avaṉatu, eṉpaṉa tuvvikutiyum
akarāccāriyiyum peṟṟu niṉṟa kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟu. eṉatu pōyiṟṟu, avaṉati
vāṅkiṉēṉ, eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟil, eṉatu, avaṉatu eṉpaṉa, mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi vanta kuṟippu
viṇiyālaṇiyum peyar. iṅṅaṇamaṉṟi, ivviṭaṅkaḷil varum atu eṉpatu āṟām
vēṟṟumiyurupaṉṟu.

213. ēḻām vēṟṟumiyiṉuṭiya urukaḷ, kaṇ, il, uḷ, iṭam mutaliyaṉavām.
ivi viṉiyiyum, viṉiyōṭu porunnum peyariyuṅ koḷḷum.

216. ciṟupāṉmi oru vēṟṟumiyurupu niṟṟaṟ kuriya viṭattē, maṟṟatoru

nākuvēyoṭu nakku vīṅku tōḷ; iṅkē aiyurupu niṟṟaṟ kuriya viṭattil ōṭurupu

īcaṟkiyāṉ vittavaṉpu; iṅkē kaṇṇurupu niṟṟaṟkuriya viṭattil kuvvurupu mayaṅkiṟṟu.

217. oru vēṟṟumip poruḷ maṟṟoru vēṟṟumi yurupōṭun takutiyāka varutalum uṇṭu.

218. ai mutaliya urupēṟkumiṭattu, yāṉ, kāṉ eṉṉun taṉmiyorumip peyarkaḷ, eṉ

peyarkaḷ, num, um, eṉavum, nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muṉṉilip paṉmip peyar, nuṅkaḷ, uṅkaḷ
eṉavum, vikārappaṭṭu varum.
utāraṇam.
niṉiṉi, uṉṉi, nummi, nuṅkaḷi, uṅkaḷi maṟṟiyurupukaḷōṭum ippaṭiyēyoṭṭuka.
tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ, eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, taṉ, tam, taṅkaḷ eṉa vikārappaṭṭu varum.

taṉmi, tammi, taṅkaḷi maṟṟiyurupukaḷōṭum ippaṭiyēyoṭṭuka.

iraṭṭāvām.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

2.2 viṉiyiyal

228. viṉic collāvatu, poruḷiṉatu, puṭip peyarcciyi uṇarttuñ collām.
puṭippeyarcciyeṉiṉum, viṉi nikaḻcciyeṉiṉum, poruntum. viṉi, toḻil eṉpavi
oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
------

----

viṉi nikaḻccikkuk kāraṇam
229. viṉiyāṉatu viṉimutal, karuvi, iṭam, ceyal, kālam, ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉṉum
ivvāṟuṅ kāraṇamāvēṇum, ivaṟṟuṭ pala kāraṇamākavēṉum, nikaḻum.
utāraṇam.
vaṉintāṉ: itterinili viṉi, viṉimutaṉ mutaliya āṟuṅ kāraṇamāka, vantatu. viṉimatal
kuyavaṉ; mutaṟkaruvi maṇ; tuṇikkaruvi taṇṭacakkara mutaliyaṉ iṭam vaṉitaṟku ātāramākiya
iṭam; ceyal vaṉitaṟku mataṟkāraṇamākiya ceyki; kālam iṟantakālam; ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṭa
mutaliyaṉa.
iruntāṉ: itterinili viṉi, viṉimutaṉ mutaliya āṟuṅ kāraṇamāka, vantatu.
uṭiyaṉ: ikkuṟippu viṉi, karuviyuñ ceyappaṭu poruḷumoḻinta nāṉkuṅ kāraṇamāka
vantatu.
-----

230. viṉi mutaṉ mutaliya āṟaṉuḷḷē, terinili viṉimuṟṟiṉ kaṇ, viṇimutaluñ ceyaluṅ
kālamumākiya mūṉṟum veḷippaṭiyākavum, maṟṟi mūṉṟuṅ kuṟippākavun tōṉṟum.
terinili viṉip peyarecca viṉiyeccaṅkaḷiṉkaṇ ceyaluṅ kālamumākiya iraṇṭum
veḷippaṭiyākavum, maṟṟi nāṉkum kuṟippākavun tōṉṟum.
viṉimutal pāl kāṭṭum vikutiyiṉālum, ceyal pakutiyiṉālum, kālam iṭiniliyum
vikutiyum vikārappaṭṭa pakutiyumākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟiṉālun toṉṟum. ecca viṉikaṭkup
pāl kāṭṭum vikuti yiṉmiyāl, avaṟṟil viṉimutal veḷippaṭat tōṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
utāraṇam.
uṇṭāṉ: itterinili viṉimuṟṟilē, pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, iṭiniliyāṟ kālamum,
vikutiyāl viṉimutalum veḷippaṭiyākavum, maṟṟavi
uṇṭa: it teriniliviṉip peyareccattilē, pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, iṭiniliyāṟ kālamum
veḷippaṭiyākavum, kuṟippākavun tōṉṟiṉa.
uṇṭu: itterinili viṉiyeccattilē, pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, iṭiniliyāṟ kālamum
veḷippaṭiyākavum, kuṟippākavun tōṉṟiṉa.
---

231. viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟikkaṇ viṉi mutaṉ māttiram veḷippaṭiyākavum, maṟṟaviyellāṅ
kuṟippākavun tōṉṟum.
viṉikkuṟippu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷiṉ kaṇ, viṉimutaṉ mutaliyavellāṅ kuṟippākavē tōṉṟum.
utāraṇam.
kariyaṉ: ikkuṟippu viṉimuṟṟilē, vikutiyāl viṉimutal veḷipṭiyākavum,
maṟṟaviyellāṅ kuṟippākavun tōṉṟum.
kariya: ikkuṟippuviṉip peyaleccattilē, viṉimutaṉ mutaliyavellāṅ kuṟippākavē
tōṉṟiṉa.
iṉṟi: ikkuṟippuviṉi viṉiyeccattilē, viṉimutaṉ mutaliyavellāṅ kuṟippākavē
tōṉṟiṉa.

----

kālam
232. kālam, iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu, eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
iṟappāvatu toḻilatu kaḻivu nikaḻvāvatu toḻil toṭaṅkappaṭṭu muṟṟup peṟāta nilami.
etirvāvatu toḻil piṟavāmi.

---

viṉiccoṟkaḷiṉ vaki
233. ikkālattōṭu pulappaṭuvaṉavākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ, teriniliviṉiyuṅ kuṟippu
viṉiyum eṉa, iruvakippaṭum.
---

234. terinili viṉiyāvatu, kālaṅkāṭṭum uruppuṇmiyiṉālē, kālam veḷippaṭat
teriyumpaṭi niṟkum viṉiyām.
utāraṇam.
naṭantāṉ: itu, takaraviṭi niliyiṉāl iṟantakālam veḷippaṭat teriyum paṭi niṟṟaliṉālē,
terinili viṉi.
uṇkum: itu, kum vikutiyiṉāl etirkālam veḷippaṭat teriyum paṭi niṟṟaliṉālē,
terinili viṉi.
peṟṟāṉ: itu, peṟu, peṟṟu eṉa vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟa pakutiyiṉāl iṟantakālam veḷippaṭat
teriyum paṭi niṟṟaliṉālē, terinili viṉi.
terinili viṉikaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkuriya mutaṉiyaṭikaḷ iviyeṉpatu pataviyalil nāṟpattāṟam
vacaṉattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
---

235. kuṟippu viṉiyāvatu, kālaṅkāṭṭum uṟuppiṉmiyiṉālē, kālam veḷippaṭat
teritaliṉṟic colluvōṉatu kuṟippiṉālē tōṉṟumpaṭi, niṟkum viṉiyām.
utāraṇam.
poṉṉaṉ: itu, poṉṉiyuṭiyaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉa iṟantakālaṅ karutiyāyiṉum,
poṉṉiyuṭiyaṉākiṉṟāṉ eṉa nikaḻkālaṅ karutiyāyiṉum, poṉṉiyuṭiyaṉāvāṉ eṉa etirkālaṅ
karutiyāyiṉun taṉṉi oruvaṉ colla, akkālam avaṉatu kuṟippāṭ kēṭpōṉukkut tōṉṟum
paṭi niṟṟaliṉālē, kuṟippu viṉi.
poṉṉaṉ eṉpatu, poṉṉuṭimi kāraṇamāka oruvaṉukkup peyarāy niṉṟu eḻuvāy mutaliya
vēṟṟumiyuru pēṟkum pōtu peyarc col; mukkālam paṟṟip puṭi peyarum oruvaṉatu
viṉi nikaḻcciyi uṇarttip peyarukkup payaṉiliyāy varum pōtu kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuc
col; aṅṅaṉam viṉimuṟṟāy niṉṟu piṉ avviṉi nikaḻcci kāraṇamāka avaṉukkup peyarāki
eḻuvāy mutaliya vēṟṟumiyurupēṟkum pōtu kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar.
kuṟippu viṉikaḷ tōṉṟutaṟ kuriya mutaṉiliyaṭikāḷ iviyeṉpatu pataviyalil nāṟpattu
nāṉkām vacaṉattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
---

236. teriniliviṉi kuṟippuviṉi eṉṉum iraṇṭum, muṟṟum, peyareccamum,
viṉiyeccamum, viṉiyeccamum eṉpaṉa mummūṉṟu vakippaṭum. eṉavē,
teriniliviṉimuṟṟum, teriniliviṉip peyareccamum, terinili viṉiyeccamum,
kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟum, kuṟippu viṉippeyareccamum, kuṟippu viṉi viṉiyeccamum
eṉa, viṉiccoṟkaḷ aṟuvakiyāyiṉa.
---

237.ivvaṟuvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷum, uṭaṉpāṭṭilum etirmaṟiyilum varum.
uṭaṉpāṭṭu viṉiyāvatu. toḻiliṉatu nikaḻcciyi uṇarttum viṉiyām. uṭaṉpāṭṭu
viṉiyeṉiṉum, poruntum.
utāraṇam.
naṭantāṉ naṭanta naṭantu
periyaṉ periya mella
etihmaṟi viṉiyāvatu, toḻil nikaḻāmiyi uṇarttum viṉiyām. etirmaṟiviṉiyeṉiṉum,
maṟiviṉiyeṉiṉum, poruntum.
utāraṇam.
naṭavāṉ naṭavāta naṭavātu
ilaṉ illāta iṉṟi
---

238. viṉiccoṟkaḷ, irutiṇiyimpāṉ mūviṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku urimiyākiyum,
palavaṟṟiṟkup potuvākiyum, vaḻaṅkum.

---

muṟṟu viṉi
239. muṟṟu viṉiyāvatu, pāl kāṭṭum vikutiyōṭu kūṭi niṟintu niṉṟu peyarik
koṇṭu muṭiyum viṉiyām.
immuṟṟuviṉi koḷḷum peyarkaḷāvaṉ povuṭ peyar, iṭappeyar, kālappeyar, ciṉippeyar,
kuṇappeyar, toḻiṟpeyar eṉṉum aṟuvakip peyarumām.
utāraṇam.
ceytāṉ cāttaṉ nallaṉ cāttaṉ
kuḷirntatu nilam nallatu nilam
vantatu kār nallatu kār
kuvintatu ki nallatuki
parantatu pacappu nallatu pacappu
oḻintatu piṟappu nallatu piṟappu

---

paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟu
240. paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟu, uyartiṇiyāpālorumip paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟum, uyartiṇip
peṇpālorumip paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟum, uyartiṇip palarpāṟ paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟum,
aḵṟiṇiyoṉṟaṉ pāṟ paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟum, aḵṟiṇip palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟum eṉa
aintu vakippaṭum.
---

241. aṉ, āṉ, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ,
uyartiṇiyāṇpālorumip paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟumām.
utāraṇam.
i. teri. ni. teri. e. teri. kuṟi avaṉ
naṭantaṉaṉ
naṭantāṉ naṭakkiṉṟaṉaṉ
naṭakkiṉṟāṉ naṭappaṉ
naṭappāṉ kuḻiyaṉ
kuḻiyāṉ
---

242. tu, ṟu, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ, uyartiṇip
peṇpālorumip paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟumām.
utāraṇam.
i. teri. ni. teri. e. teri. kuṟi avaḷ
naṭantaṉaḷ
naṭantāḷ naṭakkiṉṟaṉaḷ
naṭakkiṉṟāḷ naṭappaḷ
naṭappāḷ kuḻiyaḷ
kuḻiyāḷ
----

243. ar, ār eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ, uyartiṇip palarpāṟ
paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟumām.
utāraṇam.
i. teri. ni. teri. e. teri. kuṟi avar
naṭantaṉar
naṭantār naṭakkiṉṟaṉar
naṭakkiṉṟār naṭappar
naṭappār kuḻiyar
kuḻiyār
ceyyuḷilē palarpāṟ paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟukku, ivvikutikaḷiṉṟi, pa, mār
eṉṉam vikutikaḷum varum. avi iṭiniliyiṉṟit tāmē etir kālaṅ kāṭṭutal pataviyaliṟ
peṟappaṭṭatu.
utāraṇam.
naṭappa naṭamār - avar
ivviraṇṭaṟkum naṭappār eṉpatu poruḷ.
---

244. tu, ṟu eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ aḵṟiṇiyoṉṟaṉ pāṟ
paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟumām. ivaṟṟuḷ, ṟuvvikuti,
iṟantakāla viṭiniliyōṭaṉṟi, nikaḻkāla vetirkālaviṭinilikaḷōṭu kūṭi varātu.
utāraṇam.
i. teri. ni. teri. e. teri. kuṟi atu
naṭantatu
kūyiṟṟu naṭakkiṉṟatu
---- naṭappatu
--- kuḻiyatu
aṟṟu
ṟuvvikuti, vantaṉṟu, uṇṭaṉṟu, ceṉṟaṉṟu eṉattaṭaṟa voṟṟiṭinilikaḷiṉ muṉṉum,
pukkaṉṟu viṭṭaṉṟu, peṟṟaṉṟu, eṉa vikārappaṭaṭiṟannakālaṅ kāṭṭuṅ ku, ṭu, ṟu vīṟṟup
pakutikaḷiṉ muṉṉum, aṉcāriyi peṟṟu varum. ivi, muṟiyē, vantatu, uṇṭatu,
ceṉṟatu, pukkatu, viṭṭatu, peṟṟatu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ṟuv vikuti, kūṅiṟṟu,
ōṭiṟṟu eṉa iṉṉiṭi niliyiṉ muṉ māttiram, cāriyi peṟātu varum.
aṟṟu, iṟṟu, eṟṟu eṉpavi, cuṭṭiṉum viṉāviṉum vanta viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟukkaḷ.
ivi,
tantiṉṟu eṉa, ṟuvvikuti takaraviṭiniliyiṉ muṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟataṉṟō eṉiṉ; aṉṟu.
atu, tantaṉṟu, eṉṉum uṭaṉpāṭṭu viṉiyi maṟuttaṟkut takaraviṭinilikkum ṟuv
vikutikkum iṭiyē illeṉṉum etirmaṟiyiṭi niliyēṟṟu vanta maṟiviṉiyeṉṟaṟika.
tantiṉṟu tantatilliyeṉa poruḷpaṭum.
muṟiyē, attaṉmittu, ittaṉmittu, ettaṉmiyittu eṉap poruḷ paṭum.
ṭuv vikutiyi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic col aḵṟiṇiyoṉṟaṉpāṟ paṭarkki kuṟippu
viṉimuṟṟām, iv vikuti teriniliviṉimuṟṟiṟku illi.
utāraṇam. poruṭṭu (srī poruḷiyuṭiyatu)
ātirināṭṭu (srī ātiri nāḷiṉiṭattatu)
kuṇṭukaṭṭu (srī āḻamākiya kaṇṇiyuṭiyatu) atu
---

245. a eṉṉam vikutiyi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉiccol, aḵṟiṇip palaviṉpāl paṭarkkit
terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟumā.
ivvikuti, aṉcāriyi peṟṟum, peṟātum, varum
utāraṇam.
i. teri. ni. teri. e. teri. kuṟi. avi
naṭantaṉa
naṭanta naṭakkiṉṟaṉa
naṭakkiṉṟa naṭappaṉa
naṭappa kariyaṉa
kariya
ā eṉṉum vikutiyi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic col, aḵṟiṇip palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkki,
yetirmaṟit teriniliviṇi muṟṟām. ivvikuti kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟiṟku illi.
utāraṇam.
naṭavā -- avi
• naṭappa eṉṉum uyartiṇip palarpāṟpaṭarkkit terinili viṉi muṟṟu, vēṟu, naṭappa
eṉṉum aḵṟiṇi palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkkit terinili viṉimuṟṟum, vēṟu, muṉṉiyatu, naṭa
eṉṉum pakutiyum, pā eṉṉum, etirkāla pararpaṟpaṭarkki vikutiyumākap, pakukkappaṭṭu
varum. piṉṉiyatu, naṭa eṉṉum pakutiyum, ippeṉṉum etirkālaviṭiniliyum, ā eṉṉum
palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkki vikutiyumāka, pakukkappaṭṭu varum.

---

taṉmi viṉimuṟṟu
246. taṉmi viṉimuṟṟu, tammiyorumi, viṉimuṟṟum taṉmip paṉmi viṉimuṟṟum eṉa,
iru vakippaṭum.
---

terinili viṉimuṟṟu kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟumām.

tu ṟu eṉṉum vikutikaḷum vaḻaṅkum.

muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu
249. muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu muṉṉiliyorumi viṉimuṟṟum muṉṉilip paṉmi
viṉimuṟṟumeṉa iru vakippaṭum.
---

terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟumām.

terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟumām.

etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟu

253. etirmaṟat terinili muṟṟukkaḷ, il āl, ā, eṉṉum etirmaṟiyiṭinilikaḷōṭu pāl
kāṭṭum vikutikaḷip peṟṟu varuvaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ il iṭinili iṟantakāla iṭiniliyōṭum
vikārapṭiṟanta kālaṅkāṭṭum pakutiyōṭum. nikaḻkāla viṭiniliyōṭum. kūṭi varum. iṉi
iṭiniliyōṭu kūṭātu, il iṭinili kuñcāriyi peṟṟum āl iṭinili kuñcāriyi
peṟṟum peṟātum ākāraviṭicāriyi peṟātum etirkālam uṇartti varum.

naṭantilaṉ, peṟṟilaṉ, naṭakkiṉṟilaṉ, naṭakkilaṉ, e-m. naṭakkalaṉ, uṇṇalaṉ, e-m. naṭavāṉ,
e-m. varum. maṟṟa vikutikaḷōṭu ippaṭiyēyoṭṭik koḷka.
il, al, ā, iv mūṉṟiyum etirmaṟi vikuti eṉpar cilar. etirmaṟi iṭiniliyeṉapatē
cēṉāvariyār. civañāṉa muṉivar. mutaliyōr tuṇvu naṭavā eṉṉum aḵṟiṇippalaviṉ pāl
paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟil ākāram veru vikuti vēṇṭātu tāṉē, etirmaṟi poruḷōṭu
palaviṉpāṟ paṭarkkip poruḷiyun tantu niṟṟaliṉ, aṅku māttiram vikutiyō yeṉṟaṟika.
akaraviṭinili varumeḻuttu uyirāyavaḻik keṭutal pataviyaliṟ peṟappaṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi uṭaṉpāṭṭu terinili muṟṟukkaḷē āl eṉṉum paṉpaṭiyāka tōṉṟiya etirmaṟi
ciṟappu viṉikkuṟippōṭāyiṉum illi yeṉṉum etirmaṟitta terinili
viṉimuṟṟukkaḷāyum varum.

muṉṉiliyēval viiṉumuṟṟu

255. āy, i, āl, ēl, āl, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi irutiyil uṭiya viṉiccoṟkaḷum āy
vikuti puṇarntu kuṉṟip pakuti māttiriyāy niṟkum vici; coṟkaḷum
muṉṉiliyēvālorumi terinili viṉimuṟṟukkaḷām. ivaṟṟul al, ēṉ, āl, eṉṉum
mūṉṟu vikutikaḷum etirmaṟiyiṭattu varum.

uṇṇal uṇṇuti
uṇṇēl uṇ
māṟal nī
ēval vikutikaḷ iṭiniliyiṉṟi tāmē etirkālaṅkāṭṭal pataviyaliṟ peṟappaṭṭatu.
etirmaṟiyālorumi viṉimuṟṟukkaḷ, uṇṇātē, uṇṇātī, eṉa etirmaṟi ākāraviṭi niliyiṉ
muṉ takaraveḻuttu peṟṟōṭu ekara vikuti ikaravikutikaḷuḷ oṉṟu peṟṟum varum.

256. īr, um, miṉ, eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyil uṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ muṉṉiliyēvaṟ
paṉmit terinili viṉimuṟṟukkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
uṇṇīr, uṇṇum uṇmiṉ - nīr
etirmaniyēvaṟ paṉmiviṉi muṟṟukkaḷ uṇṇamiṉ, naṭavaṉmiṉ eṉa, pakutikkum viṉ
vikutikkum iṭiyē etirmaṟi al iṭinili peṟṟu varum.
(1) uṇṇāy, eṉṉum muṉṉiliyorumi yetirmaṟi terinili viṉimuṟṟu vēṟē: uṇṇāy
eṉṉum, muṉṉiliyēvalorumi terinili viṉimuṟṟum vēṟē: muṉṉiyatu uṇṇeṉṉum
pakutiyum peṟṟu akaraviṭinili kēṭṭu muṭinnatu. piṉṉiyatu uṉ eṉṉum pakutiyum āy
vikutiyum peṟṟu muṭintatu.
(2) uṇṇīr eṉṉum muṉṉilip paṉmiyetirmaṟi terinili viṉimuṟṟum, vēṟē: uṇṇīr
eṉṉum muṉṉiliyēvaṟ paṉmit teriniliviṉimuṟṟu vēṟē: muṉṉiyatu uṇ eṉṉum
pakutiyum āvaṉa eṉṉum etirmaṟi iṭiniliyum īr vikutiyum peṟṟu iṭinili ākāram
kēṭṭu muṭintatu. piṉṉiyatu uṇ eṉṉum pakutiyum īr vikutiyum peṟṟum muṭintatu.

peyareccam
261. peyareccamāvatu, pāhl kāṭṭum. muṟṟu vikuti peṟāta kuṟiccollāyp peyarik
koṇṭu muṭiyum viṉiyām.
ippeyareccaṅ koḷḷum peyarkaḷāvaṉ, viṉi, mutaṟpeyar, karuvip peyar, iṭappeyar,
toḻiṟpeyar, kālappeyar, ceyaṟpāṭṭup poruṭpeyar eṉṉum aṟavaki peyarumām
utāraṇam.
uṇṭacāttaṉ - viṉimutaṟpeyar
uṇṭa kalam - karuvippeyar
uṇṭa vīṭu - iṭappeyar
uṇṭa ūṇ - toḻiṟpeyar
uṇṭa nāḷ - kālappeyar
uṇṭa cōṟu - ceyappaṭu poruṭpeyar
262. teriniliviṉip peyareccam, ceyta veṉṉum vāyppāṭṭiṟantapālappeyareccam eṉavum
ceykiṉṟa veṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu nikaḻkālap peyareccam eṉavum ceyyum eṉṉum
vāyppaṭṭetirkālap peyareccam eṉavum mūvakippaṭum.
263. ceytaveṉṉum vāyppāṭṭiṟanta kām, peyareccaṅkaḷ iṟanta kālaviṭiniiyōṭu
vakārappaṭṭiṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭum takutiyōṭum akara vikuti peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.
utāraṇam.
vanta kutiri pōya kutiri
uṇṭa kutiri pukka kutiri
tiṉṟa kutiri viṭṭa kutiri
varuntiṉa kutiri uṟṟa kutiri
264. ceykiṉṟa veṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu nikaḻkāla peyareccaṅkaḷ, nikaḻkāla, viṭiniliyōṭu
akaravikuti peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.
utāraṇam.
uṇṇāniṉṟa kutiri uṇkiṉṟa kutiri
uṇkiṟa kutiri
266. etirmaṟit terinili viṉippeyareccaṅkaḷ, etirmaṟi ākāraviṭiniliyun
takaraveḻuttup pōṟṟōṭu kūṭiya akaravikutiyum peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.
ceyyāta eṉpatu ceyta, ceykiṉṟa, ceyyum eṉṉum mūṉṟaṟkum etirmaṟiyām. ivvetirmaṟi
peyareccam ceykalāta, ceykilāta, eṉa al, il eṉṉum iṭinilikaḷi ākārac cāriyiyōṭu
peṟṟu varum.
utāraṇam.
uṇṇāta kutiri naṭavāta kutiri
uṇṇāk kutiri, vaṭavāk kutiri eṉa īṟṟuyir meykeṭṭum varum.
267. kuṟippu viṉippeyareccaṅkaḷ akaravikuti peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.
utāraṇam.
kariya kutiri, periya kaḷiṟu, neṭiyavil

ākāracac cāriyiyun takaraveḻuttu peṟṟēṭu kūṭiya akara viputiyum peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.

allāta kutiri illāta poruḷ
allākkutiri illāp poruḷ eṉa īṟṟuyir mey keṭṭu varum.

viṉiyeccam
270. viṉiyeccamāvatu pāl kāṭṭum muṟṟuvikuti peṟāta kuṟice collāy
viṉiccollik koṇṭu muṭiyum viṉiyām.
ivviṉiyeccaṅ koḷḷum viṉiccoṟkaḷāvaṉa uṭaṉpāṭum etirmaṟiyum paṟṟivarum
teriniliyuṅ kuṟippumākiya viṉimuṟṟum peyareccamum, viṉiyeccamum,
viṉiyālaṇiyum, peyarum, toḻiṟpeyarum ākiya ai vaki viṉiccoṟkaḷumām.
utāraṇam.
1. teriniliviṉiyeccan terinili viṉi vikaṟpaṅkaḷ koḷḷutaṟku
utāraṇam.
uṇṭu vantāṉ; uṇṭuvārāṉ - terinili viṉimuṟṟu
uṇṭuvant uṇṭuvarāta - terinilippeyareccam
uṇṭuvantu; uṇṭuvarātu - terinili viṉiyeccam
uṇṭuvantavaṉ; uṇṭu vārātavaṉ - terinili viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar
uṇṭuvarutal; uṇṭuvarātavaṉ - terinilit toḻiṟ peyar
2. terinili viṉiyeccaṅ kuṟippuviṉi vikappaṅkaḷ koḷḷutaṟku utāraṇam:-
kaṟṟullavaṉ - kuṟippuviṉimuṟṟu
kaṟṟuvalla - kuṟippu viṉippeyareccam
kaṟṟuvallavaṉ - kuṟippuviṉiyālaṉiyum peyar
kaṟṟu vaṉmi - kuṟipput toḻiṟpeyar
3. kuṟippu viṉiyeccan teriniliviṉi vikappaṅkaḷ koḷḷutaṟku utāraṇam:-
aṟamaṉṟic ceytāṉ; aṟamaṉṟic ceyyāṉ - teriviṉimuṟṟu
aṟamaṉṟic ceyt aṟamaṉṟic ceyyāta - teripeyareccam
aṟamaṉṟic ceytu; aṟamaṉṟic ceyyāmi - teritoḻiṟ peyar
4. kuṟippu viṉiyeccaṅ kuṟippu viṉiyeccaṅ kuṟippu viṉi vikappaṅkaḷ koḷḷutaṟku
utāraṇam:-
aṟamaṉṟiyilaṉ - kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu
aṟamaṉṟiyillātu - kuṟippu viṉippeyareccam
aṟamaṉṟiyillātu - kuṟippu viṉiyeccam
aṟamaṉyillātavaṉ - kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar
aṟamaṉyiṉmi - kuṟipput toḻiṟpeyar
271. pataviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa viṉiyecca vikutikaḷum ukara vikuti iṟantakāla
viṭiniliyōṭu kūṭivarum eṉa vikuti, iṟantakālaviṭiniliyōṭum vikārappaṭṭirunta
kālaṅkāṭṭum pakutiyōṭum kūṭivarum. maṟṟi vikutiti yellām iṭiniliyiṉṟit tāmē
kālaṅkāṭṭum.
272. terinili viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ ceytu eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭiṟanta kāla viṉiyeccam eṉavum,
ceyaveṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu mukalattiṟkumuriya viṉiyeccam eṉavum ceyiṉ eṉṉum
vāypāṭṭetirkāla viṉiyeccam eṉavum mūvakippaṭum.
273. ceytu eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ, u, i, y eṉṉum vikutikaḷi
iṟutiyiṟ peṟṟu taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyē koṇṭu muṭivaṉavām.
iṅkē iṟantakālam eṉpatu muṭikkum collāl uyarappaṭum toḻiṟku viṉiyeccattāl
uṇarappaṭum. toḻilmuṉṉikaḻtali.

karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum koṇṭu muṭiyum.
kāraṇap poruḷil varutalāvatu muṭikkun collāl uṇarappaṭum toḻiṟku viṉiyeccattāl
uṇarappaṭun toḻil kāraṇam eṉpatu paṭa varutal.
utāraṇam.
maḻi peyya pukaḻpeṟṟatu - taṉkaruttāviṉ peyar
maḻi peyya nel viḷinnatu - piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉi
maḻi peyya pukaḻpeṟṟatu eṉṟaviṭattu viṉiyeccantāl uṇarappaṭum toḻili nikaḻttiṉa
viṉimutalē muṭikkuñ collāl uṇarappaṭum toḻiṟku viṉimutalātal kāṇka.
maḻipeyya nellu viḷintatu eṉaṟa viṭattu viṉiyeccat tāl uṇarappaṭum toḻili nikaḻttiṉa
viṉimutalum vēṟē: muṭikkuñ collāl uṇalappaṭum toḻili niṣikaḻttiṉa viṉimutalum
vēṟēyātal kāṇka.
ceyyuḷilē ic ceyaṉveṉvāyppāṭṭiṟantakāla viṉiyeccam eṉa eṉṉum vikutiyi peṟṟum
varum.
utāraṇam.
maḻi peyaṭateṉap pukaḻapeṟṟatu - taṉkaruttāviṉ peyar
maḻi peyteṉa nel viḷinnatu - piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉi
(4) ceyaveṉ vāyppāṭṭu viṉiyeccam etirkālattilē kariyap poruḷila vantu taṉ
karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum koṇṭu muṭiyum.
kāriyap poruḷil varutalāvatu muṭikkuñ collāl uṇalappaṭun toḻiṟku viṉiyeccattāl
uṇarappaṭum toḻil. kāriyam eṉpatu paṭa varutal.
utāraṇam.
tāṉuṇṇavantāṉ - taṉkaruttāviṉ peyar
yāṉuṇṇattantāṉ - piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉi
icceyaveṉvāyppāṭ ṭetirkāla viṉiyeccam ku eṉṉum vikutiyip peṟṟum varum.
utāraṇam.
tāṉuṇaṟku vantāṉ - taṉkaruttāviṉ peyar
yāṉuṇaṟkut tantāṉ - piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉi
uṇaṇumpaṭi, uṇṇum poruṭṭu, uṇṇum vaṇṇam, uṇṇum vaki eṉpaṉa uṇaṟkeṉṉum poruḷpaṭa
varum.
ceyyuḷilē, icceyaveṉvāyppāṭ ṭetirkāla viṉiyeccam, iya, iyar, vāṉ, pāṉ, pākku
eṉṉum vikutikaḷip peṟṟum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiya iraṇṭu vikuti peṟṟavi taṉ
karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum piṟa karuttāviṉviṉiyiyuṅ koṇṭu muṭiyum; piṉṉiya mūṉṟu
vikuti peṟṟavi taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉiyik koṇṭu muṭiyum.
utāraṇam.
iyavikuti nīrivikāṇiyavammiṉ - taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉi
avar kāṇiya vammiṉ - piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉi
iyarvikuti nāmuṇṇiyarvantēm - taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉi
nīruṇṇiyar vaḻaṅkuvēm - piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉi
vāṉvikuti - tāṉ kolvāṉ ceṉṟāṉ
pāṉvikuti - tāṉalippāṉ pukuntāṉ
pākkuvikuti - tāṉṟarupākku varuvāṉ taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉi
(5) ceyaveṉvāyppāṭṭu viṉiyeccam, taṉakkeṉa niyamamāka uriya nikaḻkālattilē, itu
nikaḻā niṟka itu nikaḻntatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa vantu, piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉiyik koṇṭu
muṭiyum.
iṅkē nikaḻkālameṉṟatu, muṭikkuñ collāl uṇarappaṭun toḻilōṭu viṉiyeccattāl
uyarappaṭun toḻil muṟpiṟ pāṭiṉṟi uṭaṉikaḻtali.
utāraṇam.
cūriyaṉutikka vantāṉ - piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉi
275.ceyiṉ eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ, iṉ, āl, kāl, kaṭi, vaḻi,
iṭattu, um eṉṉum vikutikaḷi iṟutiyiṟ peṟṟuk kāraṇapporuḷil vantu, taṉ
karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉiyiyum piṟakaruttāviṉ viṉiyiyum koṇṭu
muṭivaṉavām.
ivviṉiyeccam, etirkālac colliyē muṭikkuñ collākak koḷḷum. ivviṉiyeccattāl
uṇarappaṭun toḻil, orutaliyākavē colluvāṉ. coṟkup piṉṉikaḻvatāyum,
muṭikkuñcollāl uṇarappaṭun toḻiṟkuk kāraṇamākamuṉṉikaḻvatāyum uḷḷatu; ātalāl,
ivviṉiyeccam etihṭakālam paṟṟik kāraṇapporuḷil varuvatāyiṟṟu. orutali - tuṇivu.
(utāraṇam)
iṉ yāṇuṇṇi ṉuvappēṉ
uṇṇiṟ pacitīrum taṉkaru piṟakaru
āl nī vantāl vāḻvāy
nī vantāṉ yāṉ vāḻvēṉ taṉkaru piṟakaru
kāl nī kaṟṟakkāluvappāy
uṇṭakkāṟ pacitīrum taṉkaru piṟakaru
kaṭi nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟak kaṭiyutavum
nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟakkaṭit tīviṉi varātu taṉkaru piṟakaru
vaḻi nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟa vaḻiyutavum

iṭattu nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟaviṭattutavum
nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟaviṭattut tīviṉivarātu taṉkaru piṟakaru
um uṇṭalu muvappāy
uṇṭalum paci tīrum taṉkaru piṟakaru
vantāl eṉpatu tuccāriyi peṟṟatu. uṇṭakkāl eṉpatu tuccāriyiyum akaraccāriyiyum
peṟṟatu. uṟṟakkāl, uṟṟakkaṭi, uṟṟavaḻi, uṟṟaviṭattu eṉpaṉa akaraccāriyi peṟṟaṉa.
uṇṭalum eṉpatu tuccāriyiyum alluc cāriyiyum peṟṟatu.
uṇpāṉēl uṇpāṉeṉiṉ, uṇpāṉāyiṉ, uṇpāṉēṉum eṉa, muṟṟu viṉikaḷ, ēl, eṉiṉ,

vāyppāṭṭu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷāy varumeṉavum aṟika.
276. etirmaṟit terinili viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ etirmaṟi ākāraviṭiniliyōṭu u, mal, mē,
mi, mikku, kāl, kaṭi, vaḻi, iṭattu eṉṉum vikutikaḷip peṟṟu varuvaṉavām.
ceyyātu eṉpatu ceytu, ceypu, ceyyā, ceyyū eṉpavaṟṟiṟku, etirmaṟiyām. ceyyātu
eṉpatilē takaram eḻuttuppēṟu. ceyyātu eṉpatu, ceykalātu, ceykilātu eṉa, al il,
eṉṉum iṭinilikaḷi ākāraccāriyiyōṭu peṟṟum varum.
ceyyāmal eṉhtu, ceya eṉpataṟku etirmaṟiyām.
ceyyāmal, ceyyāmē, ceyyāmi, ceyyāmikku eṉṉum nāṉkum, ceyaṟku, ceyyiya,
ceyyiyar eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum, ceyaṟku eṉpatu paṭavaruñ ceyavēṉeccattiṟkum etirmaṟiyām.

eṉpataṟkum, apporuḷpaṭa varuvaṉavākiya ceytāl, ceytakkāl, ceytakkaṭi, ceytavaḻi,
ceytaviṭattu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum etirmaṟiyām.

280. taṉ karuttāviṉ viṉiyē koḷḷutaṟkuriya viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ ciṉi viṉiyāyiṉ, avi
acciṉiviṉiyik koṇṭu muṭitalumaṉṟi, oṟṟumipaṟṟi mutal viṉiyiyum koṇṭu
muṭiyum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉ kāloṭintu vīḻntāṉ. iṅkē oṭital ciṉiviṉi; vīḻtal mutal viṉi ātaliṉ oṭintu
eṉṉuñ ciṉiviṉiyeccam vīḻntāṉ eṉṉum mutal viṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu.
kāloṭintu vīḻntatu. iṅkē oṭitaluñ ciṉi viṉi; vīḻtaluñ ciṉiviṉi. ātaliṉ oṭintu
eṉṉuñ ciṉiviṉiyeccam vīḻntatu eṉṉum ciṉiviṉi koṇṭu muṭintatu.
māṭu kāloṭintu vīḻntatu. iṅkē vīḻtal māṭṭiṉ viṉiyātaliṟ ciṉiviṉiyeccam mutal
viṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu.
281. piṟa karuttāviṉ viṉiyik koḷḷum viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ; taṉkaruttāviṉ viṉiyikkoḷḷum
viṉiyeccaṅkaḷāka tirintum varum. tiripiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ vēṟupaṭāvām.
utāraṇam.
ñāyiṟu paṭṭu vantāṉ. iṅkē paṭa veṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ vāyppāṭṭuviṉiyeccam paṭṭu eṉa
tirintu niṉṟatu.
maḻipeytu nel viḷintatu. iṅkē peyya eṉṉuṅ kāraṇap poruṭṭākiya ceyaveṉ
vāyppāṭṭiṟantakāla viṉiyeccam peytu eṉa tirintu niṉṟatu.

282. terinili viṉimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟum,
tamakkuriya payaṉili koḷḷumiṭattu viṉiyeccattiṟkuriya payaṉili koḷḷumiṭattu
viṉiyeccap poruḷiyum, peyareccattiṟkuriya payaṉili koḷḷumiṭattu viṉiyeccap
poruḷiyum, peyareccattiṟkuriya paṇaṉili koḷḷumiṭattup peyareccap poruḷiyun tarum.
utāraṇam.
kaṇṭaṉaṉ vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ; iṅkē kaṇṭaṉaṉ, eṉṉun terinili viṉimuṟṟu, kaṇṭu eṉa viṉiyeccap
poruḷit tannatu.
uṇṭāṉcāttaṉūrkkup pōyiṉāṉ; iṅkē uṇṭāṉ eṉṉun terinili viṉimuṟṟu uṇṭaṭeṉap
peyarecca poruḷit tantatu.
uccikkūppiya kiyiṉar taṟpukaḻntu iṅkē kiyiṉār eṉṉuṅ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu,
kiyiyuṭiyaṉavarāki eṉa viṉiyeccap poruḷit tantatu.
ventiṟaliṉāṉ viral vaḻutiyōṭu; iṅkē tiraliṉāl eṉṉum kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu
tiṟaliṉaṉākiya eṉappeyareccap poruḷit tantatu.

vakippaṭum.

viruntāyiṉum tokkāyiṉum varum.

ākkaccol vēṇṭātē varum.

terinili viṉippakuppu
284. terinili viṉic coṟkaḷ, ceyappāṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiya viṉi, ceyappāṭu poruḷ,
kuṉṟāta viṉi, e-m. taṉviṉi, piṟaviṉi, e-m. ceyviṉi, ceyappāṭṭu viṉi, e-m.
vu;vēṟē vakiyiṟ pirivupaṭṭu vaḻaṅkum.
285. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉiyāvatu, ceyappāṭu poruḷi vēṇṭātu. varummutaṉili
aṭiyākat tōṉṟiya viṉiyām.
utāraṇam.
naṭantāṉ, vantāṉ, iruntāṉ, uṟaṅkiṉāṉ.
ivi, iti naṭantāṉ, iti vantāṉ eṉac ceyappaṭu poruḷēṟṟu vārāmi kāṇka.
286. ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟāta viṉiyāvatu ceyappaṭuporuḷi vēṇṭi niṟkum mutaṉili
aṭiyākat tōṉṟiya viṉiyām.
utāraṇam.
uṇṭāṉ, koṭuttāṉ, kaṇṭāṉ, paṭittāṉ, ivi, cōṟṟiyuṇṭāṉ, poruḷik koṭuttāṉ eṉac
ceyappaṭum poruḷēṟṟu, varutal kāṇka.
287. taṉviṉiyāvatu taṉṉeḻuvāy karuttāviṉ ṟoḻili uṇartti niṟkum mutaṉili
aṭiyākat tōṉṟiya viṉiyām ittaṉviṉi iyaṟṟutaṟ karuttāviṉ viṉiyeṉappaṭum.
ceyappāṭu poruḷ kaṉṟiya mutaṉili ceyappāṭu poruḷ kuṉṟāta mutaṉili eṉṉum iru vaki
mutaliṉiyum taṉviṉikku mutaṉiliyāka varum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉaṭintāṉ, taccaṉ kōyilik kaṭṭiṉāṉ.
ivikaḷilē, naṭakkiyuṅ kāṭṭalumākiya mutaṉilit toḻilkaḷ eḻuvāyk karuttāviṉ
ṟoḻilātal kāṇka.
288. piṟaviṉiyāvatu taṉṉeḻuvāy ikkaruttā vallāta piṟakaruttāviṉ toḻili uṇartti
niṟkum mutaṉiliyaṭiyāka tōṉṟiya viṉiyām. ippiṟaviṉi ēvutaṟ karuttāviṉ viṉi
eṉappaṭum.
ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiya mutaṉili, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāta mutaṉili eṉṉum iru vaki
mutaṉilikaḷum piṟaviṉi vikuti peṟṟēṉum tām vikārappaṭṭēṉum, vikārappaṭṭu
vikutipeṟṟēṉum, piṟa viṉip pakutikaḷāy, varutal pataviyaliṟ kaṭṭuvittāṉ.
ivikaḷilē naṭakkiyuṅ kaṭṭalumākiya mutaṉili toḻilkaḷ, eḻuvāy karuttāviṉ
toḻilākātu piṟakarattāviṉ toḻilātal kāṇka.
ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiya mutaṉili aṭiyākat tōṉṟiya piṟa viṉikaḷ, am mutaṉilik
karuttāvik tamakkuc ceyappaṭu poruḷāka koṇṭu varum.
utāraṇam.
koṟṟāṉ cāttaṉik kaṭippaṭittāṉ
aracaṉ ṟaccaṉāṟ kōyilik kaṭṭuvittāṉ
289. taṉ viṉikkum piṟaviṉikkum potuvāka niṟkum mutaṉilikaḷukkuñ cilavuḷavām.

290. piṟaviṉikaḷ, orōviṭattup piṟaviṉivikuti tokkum varum.
utāraṇam.
aracaṉ ceyta tēr: itilē ceyvitta eṉṉum piṟaviṉi ceyta eṉa vivvikuti tokku
niṉṟatu.
kōḻi kūlip poḻutu pularntatu: itilē kūvilittu eṉṉum piṟaviṉi kūvi eṉa
vivvikuti tokku niṉṟatu.
291. ceyviṉiyāvatu, paṭu vikuti puṇarāta mutaṉili aṭiyākat tōṉṟi, eḻuvāyk
karuttāvik koṇṭu varum viṉiyām.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉaṭantāṉ naṭappittāṉ
cāttaṉ kaṭṭiṉāṉ kaṭṭuvittāṉ
292. ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāvatu, paṭu vikuti puṇarnta mutaṉili aṭiyākat tōṉṟi,
viṉimutal mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyilum, ceyappaṭu poruḷ eḻuvāyilum varappeṟum viṉiyām.
piṟaviṉi mutaṉilikaḷum, ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟāta taṉaviṉi mutaṉilikaḷum, paṭu
vikutiyōṭum, iṭiyē akaraccāriyiyēṉum, kuccāriyiyum akaraccāriyiyumēṉum
peṟṟu, ceyappāṭṭu viṉi mutaṉilikaḷāka varum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉā limmāṭu naṭappikkappaṭṭatu
koṟṟāṉa liccō ṟuṇṇappaṭṭatu.
293. ceyappāṭṭuviṉi, orōviṭattup paṭu vikuti tokkum varum.
utāraṇam.
“ilvāḻvāṉeṉpāṉ“, iṅkē eṉappaṭuvāṉ eṉṉuñ ceyappāṭṭu viṉi eṉpāṉ eṉap paṭuvikuti
tokku niṉṟatu.
uṇṭacōṟu: iṅkē uṇṇappaṭṭa eṉṉuñ ceyappāṭṭu viṉi uṇṭa eṉap paṭu vikuti tokku
niṉṟatu.
------

viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar vikārappaṭutal
294. viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷ, ciṟupāṉmi iyalpākiyum, perumpālum vikārappaṭṭum
varum.
utāraṇam.
naṭantāṉi, kuḻiyāṉi, kuḻiyiṉaṉi, e-m. naṭantōṉ, kuḻiyōṉ, naṭantavaṉ, kuḻiyavaṉ,
e-m. naṭantaṉa, kuḻiyaṉa, e-m. naṭantavi, kuḻiyavi. e-m. varum.
tērvu viṉā - 294. viṇiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam varum.
viṉiyiyal muṟṟiṟṟu
3. iṭiyiyal
295. iṭiccollāvatu, peyarum viṉiyum pōlat taṉittu naṭakkum āṟṟal illātatāy,
appeyariyum viṉiyiyuñ cārntu varuñ collām.
tērvu viṉā - 295. iṭic vollāvatu yātu?
iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ vaki
296. iṭic col: 1. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ, 2. vikutiyurupukaḷ, 3.
iṭiniliyurupukaḷum, 4. cāriyiyurupukaḷum, 5. uvamavurupukaḷum, 6. piṟavāṟu
tattamakkariya poruḷkaḷi uṇartti varupavikaḷum, 7. oli, accam, virivu ivaṟṟik
kuṟippāl uṇartti varupavikaḷum, 8. ici niṟiyē poruḷāka niṟpavikaḷum eṉa, oṉpatu
vakippaṭum.
ivikaḷuḷ, vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ peyariyalilum, vikutiyurupukaḷum iṭiniliyurupukaḷuñ
cāriyiyurupukaḷum pataviyaliluñ collappaṭṭaṉa.
iciniṟi eṉpatu, vēṟuporuḷ uṇarttātu ceyyuḷil ōciyi niṟittu niṟpatu.
aci nili eṉpatu, vēṟu poruḷ uṇarttātu peyarccollōṭum viṉicvollōṭuñ cārttic
collappaṭṭu niṟpatu. acittal - cārttutal.
-----

uvamivurupiṭic coṟkaḷ
297. uvamivurupiṭic coṟkaḷāvaṉa, pōla, puriya, oppa, uṟaḻa, māṉa, kaṭuppa, iyiya,
ēyappa, nēra, nikara, poruva, aṉṉa, aṉiya mutaliyaṉavām.
ivikaḷuḷḷē, pōla eṉapatu mutaliya patiṉoṉṟum, iṭic collaṭiyākap piṟanta viṉiyecca
viṉikaḷa. avikaḷilē, pōl, puri, o, uṟaḻa, māṉ, kaṭu, iyi, ēy, nēr, nikar, poru
eṉṉu mutaṉilikaḷē iṭic voṟkaḷ.
aṉṉa, aṉiya eṉpavikaḷ, iṭiccollaṭiyākap piṟanta peyarecca viṉik kuṟippukkaḷ
avikaḷilē, a eṉṉu mutaṉiliyē iṭiccol aṉṉa eṉpatil ṉakaramey cāriyi: aṉiya eṉpatil
ṉakarameyyum aikāramum cāriyi.

tattam poruḷi uṇarttum iṭiccoṟkaḷ
298. piṟavāṟu tattamakkuriya poruḷkaḷi uṇartti varumeṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ, ē, o, um
mutaliyavikaḷām.
299. ēkāraviṭic col, tēṟṟamum, viṉāvum, eṇṇum piriniliyum, etirmaṟiyum
iciniṟiyum, īṟṟaciyumākiya ēḻuporuḷiyun tarum.
tēṟṟam uṇṭēkaṭavuḷ, iṅkē uṇṭeṉpataṟku
aiyamilli eṉṉun teḷivupporuḷit
tarutalāṟ ṟēṟṟam.
viṉā nīyē koṇṭāy. iṅkē nīyā koṇṭāy
eṉṉum poruḷit tarumiṭattu viṉā
eṇ nilamē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē. iṅkē nilamum nīrun
tīyum vaḷiyum eṉap poruḷpaṭa eṇṇi niṟṟal eṇ.
pirinili avaruḷivaṉē kaḷvaṉ, iṅkē oru kūṭṭatti
ṉiṉṟum oruvaṉip pirittu niṟṟalāṟ pirinili.
etirmaṟi nāṉē koṇṭēṉ. iṅkē nāṉ koḷkilēṉ
eṉṉum poruḷit tarumiṭattu etirmaṟi.
iciniṟi ’’ēyē yivalorutti pēṭiyō veṉṟār.’’ iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic ceyyuḷil
ici niṟittu niṟṟalāl ici niṟi.
īṟṟaci ’’ eṉṟumēttit toḻuvō miyāmē.’’ iṅkē
vēṟu poruḷiṉṟi iṟutiyilē cārttappaṭṭu
niṟṟalāl īṟṟaci.
300. okāraviṭic col, oḻiyiciyum, viṉāvum, ciṟappum, etihṭamaṟiyum,
teriniliyum, kaḻivum, piriniliyum, aciniliyumākiya eṭṭup poruḷiyan tarum.
ciṟappu uyarvuciṟappum iḻivuciṟappum eṉa iru vakippaṭum. uyarvuciṟappa oru
poruḷiṉatu iḻivic ciṟappittala. iṅkē ciṟappittal eṉṟatu, uyarvēyāyiṉum
iḻivēyāyiṉum iḻivēyāyiṉum ataṉatu mikutiyi viḷakkutal.
(utāraṇam)
oḻiyici paṭikkavō vantāy. iṅkē paṭittaṟkaṉṟu
viḷiyāṭutaṟku vantāy eṉa oḻinta coṟkaḷit
taruvatāl oḻiyici
viṉā kuṟṟiyō makaṉō. iṅkē kuṟṟiyā makaṉā eṉa
viṉāp poruḷit tarutalāl viṉā.
uyarvu ciṟappu oō periyaṉ. iṅkē oruvaṉatu perumiyākiya
uyarviṉ mikutiyi viḷakkutalāl uyarvuciṟappu
iḻivu ciṟappu oō koṭiyaṉ. iṅkē oruvaṉatu
koṭumiyākiya iḻiviṉ mikutiyi
viḷakkutalāl iḻivu ciṟappu
etirmaṟi avaṉō koṇṭāṉ. iṅkē koṇṭilaṉ
eṉṉum poruḷit tarumiṭattu etirmaṟi
terinili āṇō atuvumaṉṟu. peṇṇō atuvumaṉṟu
iṅkē attaṉmiyillāmiyit terivittu niṟṟalāt terinili.
kaḻivu uṟutiyuṇarātu keṭṭāri oō tamakkōruṟuti yuṇarārō eṉaṉṉumiṭattuk
kaḻivirakkapporuḷit tarutalāṟ kaḻivu. kaḻivirakkam - kaḻintataṟkiraṅkutal
pirinili ivaṉō koṇṭāṉ. iṅkē palaruṇiṉṟum oruvaṇippirittu niṟkumiṭattup
pirinili
aci nili kāṇiya vammiṉō iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic cārttappaṭṭu niṟṟalāl aci nili.
301. um eṉṉumiṭic col, etirmaṟiyum, ciṟappum, aiyamum, eccamum, muṟṟum,
eṇṇum, teriniliyum ākkamumākiya eṭṭup poruḷiyun tarum.
eccam, iṟantatu taḻīiya eccamum, etiratu taḻīiya eccamum eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
utāraṇam.
etirmaṟi kaḷavu ceyyiṉum poykūṟali yoḻika.
iṅkē kaḷavu ceyyalākātu eṉṉum poruḷit
tarutalāl etirmaṟi
uṇarvu ciṟappu kuṟavarumaruḷuṅkuṉṟam. iṅkē kuṉṟiyuṉuyarvic ciṟappittalāl uyarvuc
ciṟappu.
iḻivu ciṟappu puliyaṉum virumpāp puṉpulāl yākki. iṅkē uṭampiṉiḻivic
ciṟappittalāl aiyam.
aiyam avaṉ velliṉum vellum. iṅkē tuṇiyāmiyi uṇartalāl aiyam.
eccam cāttaṉum vantāṉ. iṅkē koṟṟaṉ vantataṉṟi eṉṉum poruḷittantāl iṟantatu
taḻīiyaveccam. iṉik koṟṟaṉum varuvāṉ eṉṉum poruḷittantāl etiratu taḻīiyaveccam.
muṟṟu ellārum vantār. iṅkē eñcāpporuḷit tarutalāl muṟṟu.
eṇ irāvum pakalum. iṅkē eṇṇutaṟkaṇ varutalāl eṇ;.
terinili āṇumaṉṟu, peṇṇumaṉṟu. iṅkē iṉṉateṉat terivittu niṟṟalāl ṟerinili
ākkam pālumāyiṟṟu. iṅke atuvē marutumāyiṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷittaruvatāl ākkam.
302.etirmaṟi viṉi aṭuttu varumiṭattu muṟṟummi eccavummiyumām.
utāraṇam.
ellārum vantilar. avar pattuṅ koṭār.
iṅkē, cilar vantār, cila koṭuppār eṉavum poruḷ paṭatalāl, eccavummiyumāyiṟṟu.
303. eccavummiyāṟ ṟaḻuvappaṭam poraṭ collil ummiyilliyāyiṉ, ac col ecca
vummiyōṭu kūṭiya coṟku mutalilē collappaṭum.
utāraṇam.
cāttaṉ vantāṉ; koṟṟaṉum vantāṉ.
iṅkē cāttaṉ eccavummiyāṟ ṟaḻuvappaṭu poruḷ
304. eṉa, eṉṟu eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭic coṟkaḷum viṉiyum, peyarum, eṇṇum, paṇpum,
kuṟippum, iciyum, uvamiyum ākiya ēḻaporuḷilum varum.
utāraṇam.
viṉi mintaṉ piṟantāṉeṉat tantiyuvantāṉ iṅkē viṉiyōṭiyintata.
peyar aḻukkā ṟeṉavoru pāvi. iṅkē peyarōṭiyinnatu.
eṇ nilameṉa nīreṉat tīyeṉa vaḷiyeṉa vāṉeṉap pūtaṅkaḷintu, iṅkē eṉṇō ṭiyintatu.
paṇpu veḷḷeṉa viḷarttatu. iṅkē paṇpō ṭiyintatu.
kuṟippu poḷḷeṉa vāṅkē puṟam vērār. iṅkē kuṟippō ṭiyintatu.
ici pommeṉa vaṇṭalampum purikuḻali. iṅkē iciyō ṭiyintatu.
uvami puli pāyteṉap pāyntāṉ. iṅkē uvamiyō ṭiyintatu.
eṉṟu eṉpatiyum ippaṭiyē ivikaḷōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka
305. mēṟkūṟiya ē, um, eṉa eṉṟu eṉṉu nāṉkiṭic coṟkaḷaṉṟiyum, eṉṟā, eṉā, oṭu,
eṉṉum immūṉṟiṭic coṟkaḷum eṉṉup poruḷil varum.
utāraṇam.
nilaleṉṟa nīreṉṟā tīyeṉṟā
nilaleṉṉā nīreṉāt tīyeṉā
nilaṉoṭu nīroṭu tīyoṭu
306. peyarc cevveṇṇum, eṇṇiṭic coṟkaḷ ēḻaṉuḷḷum ē, eṉṟā, eṉā, eṉṉu mūṉṟum,
tokic coṟ peṟṟu varum. um, eṉṟu, eṉa, oṭu, eṉṉu nāṉkum, tokic coṟ peṟṟum
peṟātum varum.
peyarc covveṇṇāvatu, peyarkaḷiṉiṭattē eṇṇiṭic coṟṟokku niṟpa varuvatu.
(utāraṇam)
cevveṇ cāttaṉ koṟṟa ṉiruvarum vantār.
ēkāraveṇ cāttaṉē koṟṟaṉē yiruvarum vantār.
eṉṟāveṇ cāttaṉeṉṟā koṟṟaṉeṉṟā viruvarum vantār.
eṉāveṇ cāttaṉeṉāk koṟṟaṉeṉā viruvarum vantār.
ummiyeṉ cāttaṉuṅ koṟṟaṉu miruvarum vantār.
eṉṟeṇ cāttaṉeṉṟu koṟṟaṉeṉaṉ ṟiruvaruḷar.
eṉaveṉ cāttaṉeṉak koṟṟaṉeṉaṉa viruvaruḷar.
oṭuveṇ cāttaṉoṭu koṟṟaṉo ṭiruvaruḷar.
ummiyeṇ cāttaṉuṅ koṟṟaṉum; vantār.
eṉṟeṇ nilaṉeṉṟu nīreṉṟu tīyeṉṟu kāṟṟeṉṟaḷavaṟu kāyameṉ ṟākiya vulakam.
eṉaveṇ nilaveṉa nīreṉat tīyeṉak kāṟṟeṉa vaḷavaṟu kāyameṉ vākiya ulakam.
oṭuveṇ nilaṉoṭu nīroṭu tīyoṭu kāṟṟo ṭavaḷaṟu kāyamoṭākiya vulakam.
307. eṉṟu, eṉa, oṭu eṉṉum im mūṉṟiṭic coṟkaḷum, eṇṇappaṭum poruṭṭōṟu
niṟṟalēyaṉṟi oriṭattu niṟkavum peṟum; appaṭi niṟpiṉum, pirintu maṟṟip
poruṭōṟum poruntum.
utāraṇam.
eṉṟeṇ viṉipaki yeṉṟiraṇṭi ṉecca niṉiyuṅkāṟ ṟīyeccam pōlat teṟum. iṅkē eṉṟeṉpatu
viṉiyeṉṟu paki yeṉṟu eṉa niṉṟa viṭattup pirintu piṟavaḻiyuñ ceṉṟu poruntiyatu.
eṉaveṇ pakipāva maccam paḻiyeṉa nāṉku - mikavāvā milliṟappāṉ kaṇ. iṅkē eṉa eṉpatu,
pakiyeṉap pāvameṉa accameṉap paḻiyeṉa eṉṟu niṉṟa viṭattup pirintu piṟavaḻiyuñ ceṉṟu
poruntiyatu..
oṭuveṇ poruḷ karuvi kālam viṉiyiṭaṉo ṭintu - miruṭīra veṇṇic ceyal. iṅkē
oṭuveṉpatu poruḷōṭu karuvi yōṭu kālattōṭu viṉiyōṭu iṭaṉoṭu eṉa niṉṟaviṭattup
pirintu piṟavaḻiyuñ ceṉṟu poruntiyatu.
308.viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ, eṇṇappaṭumiṭattu ēṟpaṉa vākiya eṇṇiṭic col viriyap peṟum,
tokāppeṟṟum, oriṭattu niṉṟu pirintu kūṭap peṟṟum varum. avi tokipeṟutalilli.
utāraṇam.
ummiyeṇ - kaṟṟuṅ kēṭṭuṅ kaṟpaṉi kaṭantāṉ
eṉṟeṉ - uṇṇaveṉ ṟuṭukkaveṉṟu vantāṉ
eṉaveṇ - uṇṇaveṉa vuṭukkaveṉa vantāṉ
cevveṉ - kaṟṟuk kēṭṭuk kaṟpaṉi kaṭantāṉ
pirintu kūṭu meṉ - uṇṇa vuṭukkaveṉṟu vanatāṉ
309. a, i, u, eṉṉu mūṉṟiṭic coṟkaḷum cuṭṭupporuḷiyum, e, ā, yā, eṉṉu
mūṉṟiṭic coṟkaḷum viṉāpporuḷiyum tarum.
utāraṇam.
akkoṟṟāṉ, ik koṟṟāṉ, ukkoṟṟāṉ
ekkoṟṟaṉ, koṟṟaṉā, yāvaṉ
310.kol eṉṉum iṭiccol, aiyamum aci niliyumākiya iraṇṭu poruḷiyun tarum.
utāraṇam.
aiyam ivvuruk kuṟṟikoṉ makaṉkol. iṟkē kuṟṟiyō makaṉō eṉṉum poruḷit tarutalāl
aiyam.
acinili kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol. iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic cārttappaṭṭu niṟṟalāl aci
nili
311. maṟṟu eṉṉum iṭiccol, viṉimāṟṟum, piṟitum, aciniliyumākiya iraṇṭu
poruḷiyun tarum.
iṅkē viṉimāṟṟeṉṟatu karutiyataṟku iṉamākiya maṟutali viṉi; piṟiteṉṟatu karutiyataṟku
iṉamākiya piṟitu.
(u-m)
viṉimuṟṟu maṟṟaṟivā nalviṉi yāmiḷiya meṉṉātu. iṅaṅkē karutiya viṉiyāvatu
nalviṉiyi viṉintaṟivām eṉpatu. ataṟku iṉamākiya maṟutali viṉiyāvatu nalviṉiyi
viriyātaṟivām eṉpatu. ataṟku iṉamākiya maṟutaliviṉiyāvatu nalviṉiyi viriyātaṟivām
eṉpatu. maṟṟeṉṟatu, iṅkē viriyātaṟivām eṉṉum maṟutaliviṉiyit tarutalāl,
viṉimāṟṟup poruḷil vantatu.
piṟitu ūḷiṟ peruvaliyāvuḷa maṟṟoṉṟu, iṅkē karutiyatāvatu ūḻoṉṟeṉpatu.
ataṟkiṉamākiya pinitāvatu ūḻallatoṉṟeṉpatu maṟṟeṉṟatu iṅkē ūḻallatoṉṟeṉṉum poruḷit
tarutalāl piṟiteṉṉum poruḷil vantatu.
aci nili maṟṟeṉṉiyāḷka. iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic cārttapappaṭṭu niṟṟalāl aci nili
312. maṉ eṉṉum iṭiccol, oḻiyiciyum, ākkamum, kaḻivum, mikutiyum, aci
niliyumākiya aintu poruḷiyum taluṣarum.
utāraṇam.
oḻiyici kūriyatōr vāṇmaṉ iṅkē irumpi aṟattuṇittatu eṉṉum oḻinta coṟkaḷit
tarutalāl oḻiyici
ākkam paṇṭu kāṭumaṉ iṅkē iṉṟu vayalāyiṟṟu eṉṉum ākkar poruḷit tarutalāl ākkam
kaḻivu ciṟiyakaṭ peṟiṉē yemakkīyu maṉṉē. iṅkē ippoḻutu avaṉ iṟantataṉāl emakkuk
koṭuttal kaḻintatu eṉṉum poruḷit tarutalāṟ kaḻivu.
mikuti enti yemakkaruḷumaṉa iṅkē mikutiyum aruḷuvaṉ eṉṉum porulit tarutalāl
mikuti
acinili atumaṟ koṇkaṉṟērē. iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic cārtatappaṭaṭu niṟṟalāl
acinili.
313. koṉ eṉṉum iṭiccol, accamum, payaṉiliyum, kālamum, permiyum ākiya nāṉku
poruḷiyun tarum.
utāraṇam.
accam koṉvāḷi iṅkē añcum vāḷi eṉṉum poruḷit tarutalāl accam.
payaṉiṉmi koṉṉē kaḻintaṉ ṟiḷami iṅkē payaṉiṉṟik kaḻintatu eṉṉum poruḷit tarutalāṟ
payaṉiṉmi.
kālam koṉvaral vaṭi iṅkē kātalar nīṅkiya kālam aṟintu varutaliyuṭiya vāṭi eṉṉum
poruḷit tarutalāṟ kālam
perumi koṉṉur tuñciṉum iṅkē periya vūruṟaṅkiṉum eṉṉum poruṉit tarutalāṟ
perumi
314. antil eṉṉum iṭiccol, āṅkeṉṉum iṭamum, aciniliyumākiya iraṇṭu
poruḷittarum.
utāraṇam.
āṅku varumē - cēyiḻi yantiṟ koḻunaṟ kāṇiya iṅkē avviṭattu varum eṉṉum poruḷit
tarutalāl āṅku.
aci nili antiṟ kaḻaliṉaṉ kacciṉaṉ iṅkē vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic cārtatappaṭaṭu niṟṟalāl
acinili.
315. maṉṟa eṉṉum iṭic col, teḷivupporuḷit tarum.
utāraṇam.
teḷivu irata.tali ṉiṉṉātu maṉṟa iṅkē taliyāka eṉṉum poruḷit tarutalāṟ ṟeḷivu.
316. amma eṉṉum iṭic col, oṉṟu colvēṉ ku; eṉṉum poruḷilum, eriyacip
poruḷilum varum.
uriyaci - kaṭṭurikkaṇ varum aci nili
oṉṟu colvēṉ kēḷ - amma vāḻi ’’tōḻi“
uriyaci - ’’ atu maṟṟamma’’
317. āṅka eṉnum iṭic col, uriyacip poruḷil varum.
utāraṇam.
uriyaci - ’’āṅkattiṟaṉalla yāṅkaḻaṟa’’
318. ār eṉṉum iṭic col, uyartaṟ poruḷilum, acinilip poruḷilum varum.
uyarttaṟporuṭṭu varum pōtu oramic collīṟṟil varum. aci niliyākum pōtu ummi
muṉṉum, ummīṟṟu viṉimuṉṉum varum.
(utāraṇam)
uyartaṟ poruḷ tolkāppapiyaṉār vantār. tanniyār vantār.
aci nili peyariṉākiya tokiyumā ruḷavē. iṅkē ār aci niliyāka ummi muṉ vantatu.
ellā vuyirōṭuñ cellumār mutalē. iṅkē ār aciniliyāka umma{ṟṟu viṉimuṉ vantatu.
319. toṟum, tōṟum, eṉṉum ivviraṇṭiṭic coṟkaḷum, iṭappaṉmip poruḷiyun toḻiṟ
payilvup poruḷiyun tarum.
utāraṇam.

toḻiṟpayilvu - paṭikkun toṟu maṟivu vaḷaṟum
tōṟum eṉpatiyum ippaṭiyē ivikaḷōṭum oṭṭik koḷka.
320. iṉi eṉṉum iṭiccol, kālaviṭaṅkaḷiṉ ellip poruḷit tarum.
utāraṇam.
kālavelli - iṉi varuvēṉ
iṭavelli - iṉiyemmūr
321. muṉ, piṉ eṉṉum iṭic coṟkaḷ, kālap poruḷiyum, iṭapporuḷiyun tantu, ēḻām
vēṟṟumip poruḷpaṭa varum.

iṭam - muṉṉiruntāṉ, piṉṉiruntāṉ
muṉ. piṉ eṉpavikaḷ, muṉpu, piṉpu, e-m.

vikārappaṭṭum vaḻaṅkum.
322. vaḷā, cummā eṉṉum iṭiccoṟkaḷ, payaṉiṉmip poruḷit tarum.
utāraṇam.
vaḷā viruntāṉ, cummā vantāṉ
323. āvatu, ātal, āyiṉum, tāṉ eṉṉam iṭic coṟkaḷ vikaṟpap poruḷit tarum.
vikappamāvatu, atu allatu itu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa varuvatu.

tarum iṭic coṟkaḷām.

coṟkaḷām.

iṭiyiyal muṟṟiṟṟu

------------------------------------------------------------------------

2.4. uriyiyal

335. uriccollāvatu, poruṭku urimi pūṇṭu niṟkum paṇpi uṇarttuñ collām.
336. ulakattup poruḷ, uyirp poruḷum, uyiral poruḷum eṉa, iru vakippaṭum.
337. ipporuḷkaḷukkuriya paṇpu, kuṇappaṇpun toḻiṟpaṇpum eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
338. uyirp poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṇappaṇpukaḷāvaṉa: aṟivu, accam, māṉam, poṟumi, mayakkam,
varuppu, veṟuppu, irakkam, naṉmi, tīmi mutaliyaṉavām.
339. uyirpporuḷkaḷiṉ ṟoḻil paṇpukaḷāvaṉa: uṇṇal, uṭuttal, uṟaṅkaḷ, aṇital, toḻutal,
naṭattal, ākkal, kāttal, aḻittal mutaliyaṉavām.
340. uyiral poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṇappaṇpukaḷāvaṉa: palvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷum, iru vakināṟṟaṅkaḷum,
aivaki niṟaṅkaḷum, aṟu vakicuvikaḷum, eṇvakip paricaṅkaḷumām.
palvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷāvaṉa: vaṭṭam, irukoṇam, mukkōṇam, catura mutaliyaṉa.
iruvaki nāṟṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa: naṟu nāṟṟam, tīnāṟṟam eṉpavikaḷām.
aivaki niṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa: veṇmi, cemmi, karumi, poṉmi, pacumi eṉpavikaḷām.
aṟu vakicuvikaḷāvaṉa: kippu, puḷippu, tuvarppu, uvarppu, kārppu, iṉippu
eṉpavikaḷām.
eṇvakip paricaṅkaḷāvaṉa: vemmi, taṇmi, meṉmi, vaṉmi, noymi, cīrmi, iḻu meṉal,
caruccari eṉpavikaḷām.
341. uyirpporuḷ, uyiral poruḷ eṉṉum iru vakip poruḷkaḷukkum uriya
toḻiṟpaṇpukaḷāvaṉa: tōṉṟal, maṟital, vaḷartal, curaṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, aṭital, naṭuṅkaḷ, olittal
mutaliyavikaḷām.
342. mēṟkūṟiya kuṇappaṇpum, uṇ, uṟaṅku mutaliya mutaṉiliyaḷaviṟ peṟappaṭun
toḻiṟpaṇpum, ākiya poruṭ paṇpi uṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ viṉic coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum.
343. ivvuric coṟkaḷ, oru kuṇattiyum pala kuṇattiyum pala kuṇattiyum uṇartti varum.
344. cāl, uṟu, tava, naṉi, kūr, kaḻi, eṉpaṉa, mikuti eṉṉum oru kuṇatti uṇarttum
uriccoṟkaḷām.
utāraṇam.
cāl - teṉmali yirunta cīrcāṉ muṉivaraṉ
uṟu - uṟuyuṉaṟan tulakūṭṭi
tava - īyātu vīyu muyir tavap palavē
naṉi - vantu naṉi varuntiṉi vāḻiya neñcē
kūr - tuṇikū revvamoṭu
kaḻi - kaḻikaṇ ṇōṭṭam
345. ceḻumi eṉpatu vaḷaṉuṅ koḻuppum eṉṉum iru kuṇatti uṇarttum uriccollām.
utāraṇam.
vaḷam - ceḻum pal kuṉṟam
koḻuppu - ceḻun taṭitiṉṟa cennāy
ivvāṟē oru kuṇattiyum pala kuṇattiyum uṇartti varum uriccoṟkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum
nikaṇṭu vāyilāka aṟintu koḷka.
-----

uriyiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
collatikāram muṟṟup peṟṟatu.
